const spaceDustParticles=[];
let lastSpaceDustFrame=0;
function drawSpaceDust(){if(!spaceDustEnabled){lastSpaceDustFrame=0;return}const width=canvas.width,height=canvas.height,targetCount=Math.max(65,Math.min(170,Math.round(width*height/14000)));while(spaceDustParticles.length<targetCount)spaceDustParticles.push({x:Math.random()*width,y:Math.random()*height,size:.45+Math.random()*1.15,alpha:.08+Math.random()*.2,vx:(Math.random()-.5)*.16,vy:(Math.random()-.5)*.12,phase:Math.random()*Math.PI*2,tint:Math.random()<.18?"#a9dfff":Math.random()<.12?"#c7b7ff":"#ffffff"});if(spaceDustParticles.length>targetCount)spaceDustParticles.length=targetCount;const now=performance.now(),delta=lastSpaceDustFrame?Math.min(50,now-lastSpaceDustFrame)/16.667:1;lastSpaceDustFrame=now;ctx.save();for(const particle of spaceDustParticles){particle.x+=particle.vx*delta;particle.y+=particle.vy*delta;if(particle.x<0)particle.x=width;if(particle.x>width)particle.x=0;if(particle.y<0)particle.y=height;if(particle.y>height)particle.y=0;ctx.globalAlpha=particle.alpha*(.72+.28*Math.sin(now*.0012+particle.phase));ctx.fillStyle=particle.tint;ctx.beginPath();ctx.arc(particle.x,particle.y,particle.size,0,Math.PI*2);ctx.fill()}ctx.restore()}

const teleportAreaGates=[];
function updateTeleportAreaGates(){
  if(activeSpecialBossArea!=="teleport"&&activeSpecialBossArea!=="gates"){teleportAreaGates.length=0;return}
  if(Math.random()<1/150)teleportAreaGates.push({x:60+Math.random()*(canvas.width-120),y:120+Math.random()*Math.max(80,canvas.height-260),life:240});
  for(let i=teleportAreaGates.length-1;i>=0;i--){teleportAreaGates[i].life--;if(teleportAreaGates[i].life<=0)teleportAreaGates.splice(i,1)}
}

function triggerHealingEffect(amount=1){if(!healingEffectsEnabled||!(Number(amount)>0))return;const now=performance.now();const count=Math.min(12,Math.max(4,Math.ceil(Number(amount)*4)));for(let i=0;i<count;i++)healingPlusParticles.push({x:Math.random()*canvas.width,y:canvas.height+20+Math.random()*45,startAt:now,duration:1575+Math.random()*525,size:126,drift:(Math.random()-.5)*55});const overlay=document.getElementById("healPulseOverlay");if(overlay){overlay.classList.remove("active");void overlay.offsetWidth;overlay.classList.add("active")}}
function spawnFloatingReward(drop){if(!damageNumbersEnabled||!drop)return;const amount=drop.amount;const labels={life:"+"+amount+" ❤️",shield:"+"+amount+" 🛡️",xp:"+"+Number(amount).toLocaleString("en-US")+" XP",ammo:"🔫",rage:"🔥"};floatingRewardNumbers.push({x:Number(drop.x)||player.x,y:Number(drop.y)||player.y,text:labels[drop.kind]||"🎁",startAt:performance.now()})}
function drawFloatingRewardsAndHealing(){const now=performance.now();if(damageNumbersEnabled){for(let i=floatingRewardNumbers.length-1;i>=0;i--){const n=floatingRewardNumbers[i],age=now-n.startAt;if(age>=900){floatingRewardNumbers.splice(i,1);continue}ctx.save();ctx.globalAlpha=Math.max(0,1-age/900);ctx.font="bold 18px Arial";ctx.textAlign="center";ctx.textBaseline="middle";ctx.fillStyle="#ffe082";ctx.shadowColor="#ffb300";ctx.shadowBlur=10;ctx.fillText(n.text,n.x,n.y-28-age*.055);ctx.restore()}}else floatingRewardNumbers.length=0;if(!healingEffectsEnabled){healingPlusParticles.length=0;return}for(let i=healingPlusParticles.length-1;i>=0;i--){const p=healingPlusParticles[i],progress=(now-p.startAt)/p.duration;if(progress>=1){healingPlusParticles.splice(i,1);continue}ctx.save();const finalPhase=Math.max(0,Math.min(1,(progress-2/3)/(1/3))),particleScale=1-.9*finalPhase;ctx.globalAlpha=1-finalPhase;ctx.translate(p.x+Math.sin(progress*Math.PI*2)*p.drift,p.y-progress*(canvas.height+70));ctx.scale(particleScale,particleScale);ctx.font="900 "+p.size+"px Arial";ctx.textAlign="center";ctx.textBaseline="middle";ctx.fillStyle="#00ff55";ctx.strokeStyle="#087a32";ctx.lineWidth=2.5;ctx.lineJoin="round";ctx.shadowColor="#00ff55";ctx.shadowBlur=28;ctx.strokeText("+",0,0);ctx.fillText("+",0,0);ctx.restore()}}
function showDamageNumber(target,damage){if(!target||!Number.isFinite(damage)||damage<=0)return;updateMirrorCloneThresholds(target,Math.max(0,(Number(target.health)||0)+damage),Math.max(0,Number(target.health)||0));if(!damageNumbersEnabled)return;const actualDamage=target.health<=0?Math.max(0,damage+target.health):damage;if(actualDamage<=0)return;const now=performance.now();let entry=floatingDamageNumbers.find(n=>n.target===target&&now-n.lastHitAt<=300);if(entry){entry.amount=Math.min(entry.startHealth,entry.amount+actualDamage);entry.lastHitAt=now;entry.startAt=now}else floatingDamageNumbers.push({target,amount:actualDamage,startHealth:Math.max(actualDamage,(Number(target.health)||0)+damage),lastHitAt:now,startAt:now});}
function drawFloatingDamageNumbers(){if(!damageNumbersEnabled){floatingDamageNumbers.length=0;return}const now=performance.now();for(let i=floatingDamageNumbers.length-1;i>=0;i--){const n=floatingDamageNumbers[i],age=now-n.startAt;if(age>=750||!n.target){floatingDamageNumbers.splice(i,1);continue}const target=n.target,size=target.boss?(target.specialType==="split"?(target.splitStage===2?38:62):55):(Number(target.size)||25);ctx.save();ctx.globalAlpha=Math.max(0,1-age/750);ctx.font="bold 19px Arial";ctx.textAlign="center";ctx.textBaseline="middle";ctx.fillStyle="#ff3030";ctx.shadowColor="#ff1010";ctx.shadowBlur=9;ctx.fillText("-"+Math.round(n.amount),target.x,target.y-size-20-age*.035);ctx.restore()}}
function spawnMeteoriteFragments(a){if(!meteoriteBreakEffectEnabled||!a||a.fragmentsSpawned)return;a.fragmentsSpawned=true;const count=10+Math.floor(Math.random()*5);for(let i=0;i<count;i++){const sides=5+Math.floor(Math.random()*3),shape=[];for(let j=0;j<sides;j++)shape.push(.58+Math.random()*.52);meteoriteFragments.push({x:a.x,y:a.y,vx:(Math.random()-.5)*7,vy:(Math.random()-.5)*7-1,rotation:Math.random()*Math.PI*2,spin:(Math.random()-.5)*.22,size:3+Math.random()*5,life:38+Math.floor(Math.random()*22),maxLife:60,shade:90+Math.floor(Math.random()*70),shape})}}
function getEnemyDeathPixelColor(enemy){if(enemy.color||enemy.visualColor)return enemy.color||enemy.visualColor;if(enemy.boss)return enemy.splitBoss?"#00ff66":"#ff1744";const colors={mirror:enemy.mirrorClone?"#d2beff":"#c9a3ff",ice:"#75e6ff",gate:"#b45cff",chaser:"#ff5733",teleport:"#d07aff",inversion:"#4fe0dc",split:enemy.splitStage===1?"#22d65d":enemy.splitStage===2?"#7cff9c":"#9c27b0"};return colors[enemy.specialType]||"#9c27b0"}function spawnEnemyDeathFragments(enemy){if(!meteoriteBreakEffectEnabled||!enemy)return;const boss=!!enemy.boss,color=getEnemyDeathPixelColor(enemy),count=boss?48:16;for(let i=0;i<count;i++){const angle=Math.random()*Math.PI*2,speed=(boss?2.4:1.7)+Math.random()*(boss?6.5:4.2),size=boss?3+Math.random()*7:2+Math.random()*4;meteoriteFragments.push({x:enemy.x,y:enemy.y,vx:Math.cos(angle)*speed,vy:Math.sin(angle)*speed-1.2,rotation:Math.random()*Math.PI*2,spin:(Math.random()-.5)*.35,size,life:boss?48+Math.floor(Math.random()*35):28+Math.floor(Math.random()*24),maxLife:85,shade:120,shape:[1,1,1,1],particleType:boss?"boss":"enemy",color})}}

function updateLowHealthHeartbeat(now){const overlay=document.getElementById("lowHealthHeartbeatOverlay");if(!overlay)return 0;overlay.dataset.pulse=heartbeatPulseEnabled?"1":"0";const ratio=player.maxLives>0?player.lives/player.maxLives:1;const impossibleAlways=difficulty==="impossible"&&maxLivesLevel===0;const intensity=Math.max(0,Math.min(100,lowHealthHeartbeatIntensity));const active=intensity>0&&maxLivesLevel===0&&gameStarted&&gameOverScreen.style.display!=="flex"&&(impossibleAlways||ratio<=.49);if(!active){if(overlay.dataset.active!=="0"){overlay.dataset.active="0";overlay.style.visibility="hidden";overlay.style.opacity="0";overlay.dataset.severity="0"}return 0}const severity=impossibleAlways?Math.max(.15,Math.min(1,.15+.85*(1-ratio))):Math.max(0,Math.min(1,(.49-ratio)/.49));const bucket=Math.round(severity*20)/20;const intensityScale=intensity/50;if(overlay.dataset.active!=="1"){overlay.dataset.active="1";overlay.style.visibility="visible";overlay.style.opacity=""}const settingKey=String(bucket)+":"+intensity;if(overlay.dataset.severity!==settingKey){overlay.dataset.severity=settingKey;overlay.style.setProperty("--heartbeat-red",String(Math.min(.99,(.58+.38*bucket)*intensityScale)));overlay.style.setProperty("--heartbeat-spread",String(Math.min(100,(12+Math.pow(bucket,.8)*48)*intensityScale))+"%");overlay.style.setProperty("--heartbeat-strength",String(intensityScale))}return 0}

let playerXPData=loadPlayerXPData();
function loadPlayerXPData(){try{const x=JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem(PLAYER_XP_KEY)||"{}");return {xp:Number(x.xp)||0,paidCategoryBonuses:x.paidCategoryBonuses&&typeof x.paidCategoryBonuses==="object"?x.paidCategoryBonuses:{},paidAllAchievementsBonus:!!x.paidAllAchievementsBonus}}catch(e){return {xp:0,paidCategoryBonuses:{},paidAllAchievementsBonus:false}}}
function savePlayerXPData(){try{localStorage.setItem(PLAYER_XP_KEY,JSON.stringify(playerXPData))}catch(e){}}
function playerLevelFromXP(total){return Math.floor(Math.max(0,total)/1000)+1}
function playerLevelProgress(total){const level=playerLevelFromXP(total),base=(level-1)*1000;return {level,into:total-base,needed:1000}}
function addPlayerXP(amount,applyDifficulty=true,reason=""){let value=Number(amount)||0;if(value<=0)return 0;if(applyDifficulty)value*=({easy:.5,normal:1,hard:1.5,super:2,impossible:3}[difficulty]||1);playerXPData.xp+=value;savePlayerXPData();return value}
function showPlayerXPGain(){renderPlayerXPUI()}
function renderPlayerXPUI(){const box=document.getElementById("playerXpSummary");if(!box)return;const pr=playerLevelProgress(playerXPData.xp);const pct=Math.min(100,pr.into/pr.needed*100);box.innerHTML=`<div class="playerXpTitle">⭐ רמת שחקן ${pr.level}</div><div>${playerXPData.xp.toFixed(2)} XP כולל</div><div>${pr.into.toFixed(2)} / ${pr.needed} XP לרמה הבאה</div><div class="playerXpBar"><div class="playerXpFill" style="width:${pct}%"></div></div>`}
function achievementTierName(tier){return tier==="common"?"common":tier==="rare"?"rare":tier==="epic"?"epic":"legendary"}
function checkAchievementCategoryBonus(tier,set){const bonuses={common:250,rare:500,epic:1000,legendary:2500,amazing:5000,ultra:0};const tierNames={common:"רגילים",rare:"נדירים",epic:"אדירים",legendary:"אגדיים",amazing:"מדהימים",ultra:"אולטרה"};if(set&&achievementDefinitions.filter(a=>a.tier===tier).every(a=>set.has(a.id))&&!playerXPData.paidCategoryBonuses[tier]){playerXPData.paidCategoryBonuses[tier]=true;savePlayerXPData();addPlayerXP(bonuses[tier],false,"השלמת כל ההישגים ה"+tierNames[tier]);}if(set&&achievementDefinitions.every(a=>set.has(a.id))&&!playerXPData.paidAllAchievementsBonus){playerXPData.paidAllAchievementsBonus=true;savePlayerXPData();addPlayerXP(100000,false,"בונוס השלמת כל ההישגים בכל הדרגות");showMessage("🏆 כל ההישגים הושלמו! +100,000 XP");}}

i=0;i<str.length;i++){h^=str.charCodeAt(i);h=Math.imul(h,16777619)}return h>>>0}function dailyPickMissions(key){const a=DAILY_POOL.slice(),picked=[];let function getAchievementState(){try{const x=JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem(ACHIEVEMENTS_KEY)||"[]");return Array.isArray(x)?new Set(x):new Set()}catch(e){return new Set()}}
function getAchievementStats(){try{const x=JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem(ACHIEVEMENTS_STATS_KEY)||"{}");return Object.assign({games:0,levels:0,meteors:0,enemies:0,bosses:0,splitBosses:0,commonCards:0,rareCards:0,epicCards:0,legendaryCards:0,amazingCards:0,specialBosses:0,impossibleBosses:0,played:{}},x)}catch(e){return {games:0,levels:0,meteors:0,enemies:0,bosses:0,splitBosses:0,commonCards:0,rareCards:0,epicCards:0,legendaryCards:0,amazingCards:0,played:{}}}}

function unlockAchievement(id){const def=achievementDefinitions.find(a=>a.id===id);if(!def)return;const set=getAchievementState();if(set.has(id))return;set.add(id);saveAchievementState(set);const reward=def.id==="ultraUnstoppable"?10000:({common:25,rare:50,epic:100,legendary:250,amazing:500,ultra:1000}[def.tier]||0);addPlayerXP(reward,false,"הישג: "+def.title);showAchievementToast(def);checkAchievementCategoryBonus(def.tier,set)}
function showAchievementToast(def){let toast=document.getElementById("achievementToast");if(!toast){toast=document.createElement("div");toast.id="achievementToast";toast.className="achievementToast";document.body.appendChild(toast)}toast.className="achievementToast "+def.tier;toast.innerHTML=`<div class="big">🏅 ${def.icon}</div><div>${GalaxyI18n.translate("הישג חדש!")}</div><div class="name">${GalaxyI18n.translate(def.title)}</div>`;requestAnimationFrame(()=>toast.classList.add("show"));clearTimeout(window.achievementToastTimer);window.achievementToastTimer=setTimeout(()=>toast.classList.remove("show"),2800)}

function countryDisplayName(code){
  try{
    const lang=(typeof GalaxyI18n!=="undefined"&&GalaxyI18n.getLanguage)?GalaxyI18n.getLanguage():navigator.language||"en";
    return new Intl.DisplayNames([lang],{type:"region"}).of(code)||code;
  }catch(e){return code}
}
function populateProfileCountrySelect(){
  const select=document.getElementById("profileCountrySelect");
  if(!select)return;
  select.innerHTML="";
  const none=document.createElement("option");
  none.value="";
  none.textContent=(typeof GalaxyI18n!=="undefined"&&GalaxyI18n.translate)?GalaxyI18n.translate("🌐 ללא מדינה"):"🌐 ללא מדינה";
  select.appendChild(none);
  COUNTRY_CODES.forEach(code=>{
    const option=document.createElement("option");
    option.value=code;
    option.textContent=countryFlag(code)+" "+countryDisplayName(code);
    select.appendChild(option);
  });
  select.value=COUNTRY_CODES.includes(profileCountry)?profileCountry:"";
}
function setProfileCountry(code){
  profileCountry=String(code||"").toUpperCase();
  if(!COUNTRY_CODES.includes(profileCountry))profileCountry="";
  if(profileCountry)localStorage.setItem(PROFILE_COUNTRY_KEY,profileCountry);
  else localStorage.removeItem(PROFILE_COUNTRY_KEY);
}

function leaderboardKey(mode){return LEADERBOARD_PREFIX+mode}
let globalLeaderboardCache=Object.fromEntries(Object.keys(difficultySettings).map(k=>[k,[]]));
let globalLeaderboardReady=false;
function normalizeGlobalLeaderboard(rows){
  const seen=new Set(),out=[];
  for(const r of Array.isArray(rows)?rows:[]){
    const playerId=String(r.player_id||"").trim();
    if(!playerId||seen.has(playerId))continue;
    seen.add(playerId);
    out.push({
      playerId,
      name:String(r.player_name||"שחקן").slice(0,20),
      countryCode:String(r.country_code||"").toUpperCase(),
      distance:Number(r.distance)||0,
      level:Number(r.level)||1,
      date:r.created_at?Date.parse(r.created_at)||Date.now():Date.now(),
      achievements:Array.isArray(r.achievements)?r.achievements:[]
    });
  }
  return out.sort((a,b)=>Number(b.distance)-Number(a.distance)||Number(b.level)-Number(a.level)||Number(a.date)-Number(b.date)).slice(0,100);
}
function getLeaderboard(mode){
  const list=globalLeaderboardCache[mode];
  return Array.isArray(list)?list.slice():[];
}
async function loadGlobalLeaderboard(mode){
  if(!supabaseClient)return [];
  try{
    const {data,error}=await supabaseClient.from("leaderboard")
      .select("id,player_id,player_name,country_code,distance,level,difficulty,created_at,achievements")
      .eq("difficulty",mode)
      .order("distance",{ascending:false})
      .order("level",{ascending:false})
      .order("created_at",{ascending:true})
      .limit(500);
    if(error)throw error;
    const list=normalizeGlobalLeaderboard(data);
    globalLeaderboardCache[mode]=list;
    globalLeaderboardReady=true;
    return list;
  }catch(e){
    console.warn("Global leaderboard unavailable.",e);
    globalLeaderboardCache[mode]=[];
    return [];
  }
}
async function loadAllGlobalLeaderboards(){
  await Promise.all(Object.keys(difficultySettings).map(loadGlobalLeaderboard));
  await updateGlobalPlayerCount();
  renderLeaderboard(difficulty);
}
function getCountryCode(){
  try{
    const locale=new Intl.Locale(navigator.language||"").region;
    if(locale&&/^[A-Z]{2}$/.test(locale))return locale;
  }catch(e){}
  const lang=String(navigator.language||"").toLowerCase().split("-")[0];
  return ({he:"IL",en:"US",es:"ES",fr:"FR",de:"DE",ru:"RU",ar:"SA"}[lang]||"");
}
function countryFlag(code){
  const c=String(code||"").toUpperCase();
  if(!/^[A-Z]{2}$/.test(c))return "🌐";
  return String.fromCodePoint(...[...c].map(ch=>127397+ch.charCodeAt(0)));
}
function translatePlayerName(name){
  const raw=String(name||"שחקן").trim()||"שחקן";
  return raw.replace(/שחקן/g,()=>GalaxyI18n.translate("שחקן"));
}
function randomPlayerName(){
  return "שחקן "+(100+Math.floor(Math.random()*900));
}
async function isPlayerNameTaken(name){
  const normalized=String(name||"").trim().toLocaleLowerCase();
  if(!normalized)return true;
  const localTaken=getLeaderboard(difficulty).some(e=>String(e.name||"").trim().toLocaleLowerCase()===normalized&&e.playerId!==playerProfileId);
  if(localTaken)return true;
  if(!supabaseClient)return false;
  try{
    const {data,error}=await supabaseClient.from("player_registry").select("player_id,player_name").limit(1000);
    if(error)throw error;
    return (data||[]).some(row=>String(row.player_name||"").trim().toLocaleLowerCase()===normalized&&String(row.player_id||"")!==playerProfileId);
  }catch(e){console.warn("Player name availability check failed.",e);return false}
}
async function generateAvailablePlayerName(){
  for(let i=0;i<30;i++){
    const candidate=randomPlayerName();
    if(!(await isPlayerNameTaken(candidate)))return candidate;
  }
  for(let digits=4;digits<=8;digits++){
    const min=10**(digits-1),max=10**digits-1;
    for(let i=0;i<30;i++){
      const candidate="שחקן "+(min+Math.floor(Math.random()*(max-min+1)));
      if(!(await isPlayerNameTaken(candidate)))return candidate;
    }
  }
  return "שחקן "+Date.now();
}
async function registerGlobalPlayer(){
  if(!supabaseClient)return true;
  try{
    const name=(String(profileName||"שחקן").trim()||"שחקן").slice(0,20);
    const {error}=await supabaseClient.from("player_registry").upsert({
      player_id:playerProfileId,
      player_name:name,
      country_code:getCountryCode()||null,
      last_seen:new Date().toISOString()
    },{onConflict:"player_id"});
    if(error)throw error;
    return true;
  }catch(e){console.warn("Global player registration failed.",e);return false}
}
async function getGlobalPlayerCount(){
  if(!supabaseClient)return null;
  try{
    const {count,error}=await supabaseClient.from("player_registry").select("player_id",{count:"exact",head:true});
    if(error)throw error;
    return Number(count)||0;
  }catch(e){console.warn("Global player count unavailable.",e);return null}
}
async function updateGlobalPlayerCount(){
  const el=document.getElementById("globalPlayerCount");if(!el)return;
  const count=await getGlobalPlayerCount();
  if(count!==null)el.textContent="👥 שחקנים ששיחקו אי פעם: "+count;
}
function getStats(){try{const x=JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem(STATS_KEY)||"{}");return {totalTime:Number(x.totalTime)||0,games:Number(x.games)||0,played:x.played&&typeof x.played==="object"?x.played:{}}}catch(e){return {totalTime:0,games:0,played:{}}}}
function saveStatsForGame(){const st=getStats();st.totalTime+=Math.max(0,Math.floor(gameTime));st.games++;st.played[difficulty]=(Number(st.played[difficulty])||0)+1;try{localStorage.setItem(STATS_KEY,JSON.stringify(st))}catch(e){}}

async function syncProfileToLeaderboard(){
  if(!supabaseClient)return true;
  const name=(String(profileName||"שחקן").trim()||"שחקן").slice(0,20);
  const country=COUNTRY_CODES.includes(profileCountry)?profileCountry:null;
  try{
    const registry=await supabaseClient.from("player_registry").upsert({
      player_id:playerProfileId,
      player_name:name,
      country_code:country,
      last_seen:new Date().toISOString()
    },{onConflict:"player_id"});
    if(registry.error)throw registry.error;
    const leaderboardUpdate=await supabaseClient.from("leaderboard").update({
      player_name:name,
      country_code:country
    }).eq("player_id",playerProfileId);
    if(leaderboardUpdate.error)throw leaderboardUpdate.error;
    Object.keys(globalLeaderboardCache).forEach(mode=>{
      globalLeaderboardCache[mode]=(globalLeaderboardCache[mode]||[]).map(entry=>entry.playerId===playerProfileId?{...entry,name,countryCode:country||""}:entry);
    });
    return true;
  }catch(e){
    console.warn("Profile sync failed.",e);
    return false;
  }
}
function getPersonalBest(mode=difficulty){
  const list=getLeaderboard(mode);
  const mine=list.find(x=>x.playerId===playerProfileId);
  return mine?Number(mine.distance)||0:0;
}
function checkLiveRecord(){
  if(!gameStarted||!gameRunning||recordBroken)return;
  const d=Math.floor(distance);
  if(personalBestAtStart>0&&d>personalBestAtStart){
    recordBroken=true;
    showMessage("🏆 שיא חדש! "+d+"m");
    const banner=document.getElementById("recordLiveBanner");
    if(banner){banner.textContent="🏆 שיא חדש! "+d+"m";banner.style.display="block";clearTimeout(window.recordBannerTimer);window.recordBannerTimer=setTimeout(()=>banner.style.display="none",2200)}
  }
}
async function requestRecordName(){
  if(!recordNameOverlay||!recordNameInput)return true;
  const suggested=await generateAvailablePlayerName();
  recordNameInput.value=profileName&&profileName!=="שחקן" ? profileName : suggested;
  recordNameError.textContent="";
  recordNameOverlay.style.display="flex";
  recordNameInput.focus();
  recordNameInput.select();
  return new Promise(resolve=>{
    const submit=async()=>{
      const name=(recordNameInput.value||"").trim().slice(0,20);
      if(!name){recordNameError.textContent=GalaxyI18n.translate("יש להזין שם.");return}
      saveRecordNameButton.disabled=true;
      recordNameError.textContent=GalaxyI18n.translate("בודק אם השם פנוי…");
      const taken=await isPlayerNameTaken(name);
      if(taken){recordNameError.textContent=GalaxyI18n.translate("השם הזה כבר תפוס. בחר שם אחר.");saveRecordNameButton.disabled=false;recordNameInput.focus();return}
      setProfileName(name);
      const registered=await registerGlobalPlayer();
      if(!registered){recordNameError.textContent=GalaxyI18n.translate("לא ניתן לשמור את השם כרגע. נסה שוב.");saveRecordNameButton.disabled=false;return}
      recordNameOverlay.style.display="none";
      saveRecordNameButton.disabled=false;
      resolve(true);
    };
    saveRecordNameButton.onclick=submit;
    recordNameInput.onkeydown=e=>{if(e.key==="Enter"){e.preventDefault();submit()}};
  });
}
async function saveLeaderboardScore(){
  if(scoreSaved)return -1;
  scoreSaved=true;
  if(!supabaseClient)return -1;
  const name=(String(profileName||"שחקן").trim()||"שחקן").slice(0,20);
  const entry={
    player_id:playerProfileId,
    player_name:name,
    score:Math.floor(distance),
    distance:Math.floor(distance),
    level:Number(level),
    difficulty,
    achievements:achievementSnapshot()
  };
  try{
    const {error}=await supabaseClient.from("leaderboard").insert(entry);
    if(error)throw error;
    const list=await loadGlobalLeaderboard(difficulty);
    const rank=list.findIndex(x=>x.playerId===playerProfileId&&Number(x.distance)===entry.distance);
    return rank>=0&&rank<100?rank:-1;
  }catch(e){
    console.warn("Global leaderboard save failed.",e);
    return -1;
  }
}
function renderLeaderboard(mode=difficulty){
  leaderboardTabs.innerHTML="";
  Object.keys(difficultySettings).forEach(key=>{const btn=document.createElement("button");btn.type="button";btn.textContent=leaderboardDifficultyNames[key];if(key===mode)btn.classList.add("active");btn.addEventListener("click",()=>renderLeaderboard(key));leaderboardTabs.appendChild(btn)});
  const list=getLeaderboard(mode);leaderboardBody.innerHTML="";
  if(!list.length){leaderboardBody.innerHTML='<tr><td colspan="4">אין עדיין תוצאות. היה הראשון!</td></tr>';return}
  list.forEach((entry,i)=>{const tr=document.createElement("tr");const tdName=document.createElement("td");const b=document.createElement("button");b.type="button";b.className="leaderboardUser";b.textContent=countryFlag(entry.countryCode)+" "+translatePlayerName(entry.name||"שחקן");b.addEventListener("click",()=>openPublicProfile(entry.playerId,entry.name||"שחקן"));tdName.appendChild(b);tr.appendChild(Object.assign(document.createElement("td"),{textContent:String(i+1)}));tr.appendChild(tdName);tr.appendChild(Object.assign(document.createElement("td"),{textContent:"📏 "+Math.floor(entry.distance)+"m"}));tr.appendChild(Object.assign(document.createElement("td"),{textContent:"⭐ "+Math.floor(entry.level||1)}));leaderboardBody.appendChild(tr)});
}

function formatDuration(seconds){const total=Math.max(0,Math.floor(seconds)),d=Math.floor(total/86400),h=Math.floor(total%86400/3600),m=Math.floor(total%3600/60),sec=total%60;return (d?d+" ימים ":"")+(h?String(h).padStart(2,"0")+":":"")+String(m).padStart(2,"0")+":"+String(sec).padStart(2,"0")}

function escapeHtml(value){
  return String(value).replace(/[&<>"']/g,c=>({"&":"&amp;","<":"&lt;",">":"&gt;",'"':"&quot;","'":"&#039;"}[c]));
}
function formatTime(seconds){const total=Math.floor(seconds),h=Math.floor(total/3600),m=Math.floor(total%3600/60),sec=total%60;return (h?String(h).padStart(2,"0")+":":"")+String(m).padStart(2,"0")+":"+String(sec).padStart(2,"0")}

function collectionId(item){return item.id||((item.boss?"boss:":"entity:")+item.title)}
function collectionIdForEntity(entity,isAsteroid){
  if(entity&&entity.boss){
    const t=entity.specialType||"normal";
    return t==="normal"?"boss:בוס רגיל":({mirror:"boss:בוס המראה",ice:"boss:בוס הקרח",gates:"boss:בוס השערים",chaser:"boss:בוס הרודף",teleport:"boss:בוס הטלפורט",inversion:"boss:בוס ההיפוך",split:"boss:בוס מתפצל",king:"boss:מלך החלל",doom:"boss:בוס האבדון"}[t]||null);
  }
  if(isAsteroid){
    const t=entity.specialType||"normal";
    return ({normal:"entity:מטאוריט",mirror:"entity:מטאוריט שיבוט",ice:"entity:מטאוריט קפוא",gate:"entity:מטאוריט שער",chaser:"entity:מטאוריט רודף",chaserMeteor:"entity:מטאוריט רודף",teleport:"entity:מטאוריט טלפורט",inversion:"entity:מטאוריט הפוך",split:"entity:מטאוריט מתפצל"}[t]||"entity:מטאוריט");
  }
  const t=entity.specialType||"normal";
  return ({normal:"entity:חללית אויב",mirror:"entity:חללית שיבוט",ice:"entity:אויב קפוא",gate:"entity:שומר שער",gates:"entity:שומר שער",chaser:"entity:רודף קטן",teleport:"entity:טלפורטר",inversion:"entity:חללית הפוכה",split:"entity:אויב מתפצל",doom:"entity:שליח האבדון"}[t]||"entity:חללית אויב");
}

const COLLECTION_DATA=[
  {section:"🌌 האזור הרגיל",items:[

function renderCollection(){
  collectionContent.innerHTML="";
  COLLECTION_DATA.forEach(section=>{
    const wrap=document.createElement("section");wrap.className="collectionSection";
    const h=document.createElement("h2");h.textContent=GalaxyI18n.translate(section.section);wrap.appendChild(h);
    const grid=document.createElement("div");grid.className="collectionGrid";
    section.items.forEach(item=>{
      const unlocked=collectionUnlocks.has(item.id);
      const card=document.createElement("div");
      card.className="collectionCard"+(item.boss?" boss":"")+(unlocked?"":" locked");
      card.innerHTML=unlocked?`<div class="icon">${item.icon}</div><div class="title">${GalaxyI18n.translate(item.title)}</div><div class="desc">${GalaxyI18n.translate(item.desc)}</div>`:`<div class="icon">❓</div><div class="title">???</div><div class="desc">${GalaxyI18n.translate("כדי לפתוח צריך להרוס את האויב/המטאוריט/הבוס הזה במשחק.")}</div>`;
      grid.appendChild(card);
    });
    wrap.appendChild(grid);collectionContent.appendChild(wrap);
  });
}

saveProfileNameButton.addEventListener("click",async e=>{
  e.preventDefault();e.stopPropagation();
  const newName=(String(profileNameInput.value||"").trim()||"שחקן").slice(0,16);
  const newCountry=String(document.getElementById("profileCountrySelect")?.value||"").toUpperCase();
  if(await isPlayerNameTaken(newName)){
    showMessage(GalaxyI18n.translate("השם הזה כבר תפוס. בחר שם אחר."));
    return;
  }
  const oldName=profileName;
  const oldCountry=profileCountry;
  setProfileName(newName);
  setProfileCountry(newCountry);
  const synced=await syncProfileToLeaderboard();
  if(!synced){
    setProfileName(oldName);
    setProfileCountry(oldCountry);
    profileNameInput.value=oldName;
    populateProfileCountrySelect();
    showMessage(GalaxyI18n.translate("לא ניתן לשמור את השם כרגע. נסה שוב."));
    return;
  }
  profileNameInput.value=profileName;
  populateProfileCountrySelect();
  renderProfile();renderAchievements();
  showMessage("✅ "+GalaxyI18n.translate("הפרופיל נשמר!"));
});

function getDistanceZone(){
  const d=Math.floor(distance);
  return distanceZones.find(z=>d>=z.min&&d<z.max)||distanceZones[distanceZones.length-1];
}
function getZoneColors(mode,zone){
  const palettes={
    easy:{normal:["#062b15","#02030a"],danger:["#064d28","#02030a"],nebula:["#063d52","#02030a"],deadly:["#183b18","#02030a"],dark:["#06100b","#000000"]},
    normal:{normal:["#101b3d","#02030a"],danger:["#4b1b12","#02030a"],nebula:["#35145e","#02030a"],deadly:["#3b0909","#02030a"],dark:["#09090f","#000000"]},
    hard:{normal:["#3b1200","#02030a"],danger:["#681700","#02030a"],nebula:["#4b155f","#02030a"],deadly:["#5a0505","#000000"],dark:["#170005","#000000"]},
    super:{normal:["#350008","#02030a"],danger:["#5a000b","#02030a"],nebula:["#3d075d","#02030a"],deadly:["#4d0008","#000000"],dark:["#120006","#000000"]},
    impossible:{normal:["#000000","#000000"],danger:["#07000b","#000000"],nebula:["#10001c","#000000"],deadly:["#120006","#000000"],dark:["#000000","#000000"]}
  };
  return (palettes[mode]||palettes.normal)[zone]||palettes.normal.normal;
}

function setSpecialBossArea(type,active=true){
  const area=active?(SPECIAL_BOSS_AREAS[type]||SPECIAL_BOSS_AREAS.split):null;
  activeSpecialBossArea=active?type:"";
  if(area){
    const gradient="radial-gradient(circle at 50% 40%, "+area.colors[0]+", "+area.colors[1]+" 82%)";
    document.body.style.background=area.colors[1];
    canvas.style.background=gradient;
    const indicator=document.getElementById("zoneIndicator");
    if(indicator){indicator.textContent=area.name;indicator.style.color=area.text;indicator.style.borderColor=area.text;}
  }else{
    document.body.style.background=difficultySettings[difficulty].background;
    canvas.style.background="radial-gradient(circle at 50% 40%, "+difficultySettings[difficulty].background+", #02030a 80%)";
    const indicator=document.getElementById("zoneIndicator");
    if(indicator){indicator.textContent="🌌 חלל רגיל";indicator.style.color=difficultySettings[difficulty].color||"#fff";indicator.style.borderColor=difficultySettings[difficulty].color||"#fff";}
  }
}

function setJoystickFromEvent(e){
  const r=joystickArea.getBoundingClientRect();
  const cx=r.left+r.width/2,cy=r.top+r.height/2;
  let dx=e.clientX-cx,dy=e.clientY-cy;
  const max=58,len=Math.hypot(dx,dy)||1;
  if(len>max){dx=dx/len*max;dy=dy/len*max}
  joystickX=dx/max;joystickY=dy/max;
  joystickStick.style.transform=`translate(${dx}px,${dy}px)`;
  keys.arrowleft=joystickX<-.22;keys.arrowright=joystickX>.22;
  keys.arrowup=joystickY<-.22;keys.arrowdown=joystickY>.22;
}

}
function resetJoystick(){
  joystickPointerId=null;joystickX=0;joystickY=0;
  joystickStick.style.transform="translate(0,0)";
  keys.arrowleft=keys.arrowright=keys.arrowup=keys.arrowdown=false;
}

}

function speedComboStepMeters(){
  if(difficulty==="easy")return 200;
  if(difficulty==="normal")return 100;
  if(difficulty==="hard")return 50;
  if(difficulty==="super")return 50;
  if(difficulty==="impossible")return 10;
  return Infinity;
}
function speedComboStepValue(){return difficulty==="super"?.02:.01}
function speedComboMultiplier(){return Math.min(10,1+speedComboPermanentBonus+speedComboTemporaryBonus)}
function worldSpeedMultiplier(){return speedComboMultiplier()}
function rageActive(){return lowHealthActive()||deadlyLootRageActive()}
function effectiveMoveSpeed(){let v=player.speed;if(rageActive())v*=1.2;if(freezeTimer>0)v*=.5;if(ultraModeActive)v*=1.2;if(ultraLaserActive&&ultraModeActive)v*=.5;return v}
function resetTemporarySpeedCombo(){
  // A hit resets ONLY the non-fixed combo.
  // The permanent combo and its distance counter are intentionally untouched.
  speedComboDistance=0;
  speedComboTemporaryBonus=0;
  speedComboLastAwardedStep=0;
  updateSpeedComboUI();
}
function updateSpeedComboUI(){
  const hud=document.getElementById("speedComboHud");
  const legacy=document.getElementById("speedComboText");
  const enabled=Number.isFinite(speedComboStepMeters());
  const text="×"+speedComboMultiplier().toFixed(2);
  if(hud){hud.textContent=text;hud.style.display=enabled?"":"none"}
  if(legacy){legacy.textContent=text;legacy.style.display="none"}
}
function updateSpeedComboDistance(delta){
  const step=speedComboStepMeters();
  if(!Number.isFinite(step)||delta<=0)return;

  // Permanent/fixed combo:
  // tracks total run distance and NEVER resets when the player is hit.
  const permanentPrevious=Math.floor(speedComboPermanentDistance/step);
  speedComboPermanentDistance+=delta;
  const permanentCurrent=Math.floor(speedComboPermanentDistance/step);
  if(permanentCurrent>permanentPrevious){
    speedComboPermanentBonus=Math.min(
      9,
      speedComboPermanentBonus+(permanentCurrent-permanentPrevious)*speedComboStepValue()
    );
    speedComboPermanentLastAwardedStep=permanentCurrent;
  }

  // Temporary combo:
  // tracks only the distance travelled since the last hit and DOES reset on hit.
  const previous=Math.floor(speedComboDistance/step);
  speedComboDistance+=delta;
  const current=Math.floor(speedComboDistance/step);
  if(current>previous){
    speedComboTemporaryBonus=Math.min(
      9-speedComboPermanentBonus,
      speedComboTemporaryBonus+(current-previous)*speedComboStepValue()
    );
    speedComboLastAwardedStep=current;
  }

  if(permanentCurrent>permanentPrevious||current>previous)updateSpeedComboUI();
}
function addBossSpeedCombo(){
  const bonus={easy:.05,normal:.05,hard:.10,super:.15,impossible:.20}[difficulty]||0;
  // Permanent combo: increases from boss rewards and is NEVER changed by damagePlayer/resetTemporarySpeedCombo.
  speedComboPermanentBonus=Math.min(9,speedComboPermanentBonus+bonus);
  updateSpeedComboUI();
}
function shoot(){if(!gameRunning||paused||(levelUpOpen&&!canControlDuringLevelUp()))return;if(currentWeapon==="laser"||currentWeapon==="flamethrower"||currentWeapon==="infernoBomb")return;if(currentWeapon==="blackHole"){if(player.lastShot>0){player.lastShot--;return}if(blackHoleAmmo<=0){showMessage("🌌 נגמרו יריות חור שחור!");return}shootBlackHole();blackHoleAmmo--;ammo.blackHole=blackHoleAmmo;player.lastShot=Math.max(24,48-fireRateLevel*2);updateHUD();return;}if(currentWeapon==="rpg"){if(performance.now()<rpgCooldownUntil)return;if(ammo.rpg<=0){showMessage("🚀 נגמרה תחמושת ה־RPG!");return}shootRPG();ammo.rpg--;rpgCooldownUntil=performance.now()+getEffectiveRpgCooldownMs(fireRateLevel);updateHUD();return}if(currentWeapon!=="pistol"&&ammo[currentWeapon]!==Infinity&&ammo[currentWeapon]<=0){showMessage("🎯 נגמרה התחמושת!");return}if(player.lastShot>0){player.lastShot--;return}if(currentWeapon==="shotgun"){shootShotgun();ammo.shotgun--}else shootPistol();player.lastShot=Math.max(2,effectiveFireRate()-fireRateLevel*2);updateHUD()}
function createBullet(angle,speed,damage,size,extra={}){bullets.push({x:player.x,y:player.y-30,vx:Math.cos(angle)*speed,vy:Math.sin(angle)*speed,damage,size,pierceRemaining:piercingLevel,hitTargets:[],piercingHits:[],homing:homingLevel>0,splitGeneration:0,splitTravel:0,...extra})}
function getPiercingHit(b,target){
  if(!b||!target)return null;
  if(!Array.isArray(b.piercingHits))b.piercingHits=[];
  if(!Array.isArray(b.hitTargets))b.hitTargets=[];
  let entry=b.piercingHits.find(hit=>hit.target===target);
  const maxHits=Math.max(1,Math.min(6,(Number(piercingLevel)||0)+1));
  const damageByLevel=[[1],[1,.5],[1,.6,.4],[1,.7,.45,.3],[1,.8,.55,.35,.2],[1,.9,.6,.4,.25,.15]];
  const levelIndex=Math.max(0,Math.min(5,Number(piercingLevel)||0));
  if(!entry){
    const isFinalTarget=(Number(b.pierceRemaining)||0)<=0;
    if(!b.hitTargets.includes(target))b.hitTargets.push(target);
    if(!isFinalTarget)b.pierceRemaining--;
    entry={target,hits:0,killAfterHits:isFinalTarget};
    b.piercingHits.push(entry);
  }
  if(entry.hits>=maxHits)return null;
  const multiplier=(damageByLevel[levelIndex]||damageByLevel[5])[entry.hits]??.15;
  entry.hits++;
  return {multiplier,deadAfterHit:entry.killAfterHits&&entry.hits>=maxHits};
}
function getSplitBulletCount(){return Math.max(0,splitBulletLevel+1)}
function getSplitBulletSpread(){return (20+Math.max(0,splitBulletLevel-1)*5)*Math.PI/180}
function getSplitBulletSpeedMultiplier(){return 1+Math.max(0,Math.min(9,splitBulletLevel-1))*.1}
function getSplitBulletRangeMultiplier(){return getSplitBulletSpeedMultiplier()}
function getSplitBulletSizeMultiplier(){return 1+Math.max(0,Math.min(9,splitBulletLevel-1))*.05}
function getSplitBulletAlpha(generation){return generation<=0?1:generation===1?.75:generation===2?.5:.25}
function shouldSplitBullet(b){
  if(splitBulletLevel<=0||!b||b.rpg||b.blackHole)return false;
  const generation=Math.max(0,Number(b.splitGeneration)||0);
  if(generation>=3)return false;
  if(generation===0)return true;
  const chance=(generation===1?.05:.01)*Math.min(10,splitBulletLevel);
  return Math.random()<chance;
}
function spawnSplitBullets(b){
  const levelValue=Math.max(1,splitBulletLevel),count=getSplitBulletCount(),generation=Math.min(3,(Number(b.splitGeneration)||0)+1);
  if(!count||generation>3)return;
  const baseAngle=Math.atan2(b.vy,b.vx),spread=getSplitBulletSpread(),speed=Math.hypot(b.vx,b.vy)*getSplitBulletSpeedMultiplier(),childDamage=b.damage*levelValue/count,childSize=Math.max(2,b.size*getSplitBulletSizeMultiplier()),maxTravel=900*getSplitBulletRangeMultiplier();
  for(let i=0;i<count;i++){
    const t=count===1?.5:i/(count-1),angle=baseAngle+(-spread+spread*2*t);
    bullets.push({x:b.x,y:b.y,vx:Math.cos(angle)*speed,vy:Math.sin(angle)*speed,damage:childDamage,size:childSize,piercing:false,homing:homingLevel>0,splitGeneration:generation,splitTravel:0,splitMaxTravel:maxTravel,splitChild:true});
  }
}
function shootPistol(){const count=1+extraFireLevel+(extraRowLevel>0?1:0);for(let i=0;i<count;i++){const offset=(i-(count-1)/2)*.12;createBullet(-Math.PI/2+offset,9,effectiveDamage(),5+bulletSizeLevel)}}

function shootRPG(){createBullet(-Math.PI/2,4,effectiveDamage()*5,13+bulletSizeLevel,{rpg:true,piercing:false})}
function shootShotgun(){const count=6+extraFireLevel;for(let i=0;i<count;i++){const angle=-Math.PI/2+(i-(count-1)/2)*.55/Math.max(1,count-1);createBullet(angle,9,effectiveDamage()*.5,5+bulletSizeLevel)}}
function shootBlackHole(){createBullet(-Math.PI/2,5,effectiveDamage()*7,15+bulletSizeLevel,{blackHole:true,piercing:false,homing:false})}
function triggerChainLightning(source){if(chainLightningLevel<=0)return;const maxJumps=2+chainLightningLevel,range=120+chainLightningLevel*10,targets=[...asteroids,...enemies].filter(t=>t.health>0);let current=source,used=new Set([source]);for(let i=0;i<maxJumps;i++){let next=null,best=Infinity;for(const t of targets){if(used.has(t))continue;const d=Math.hypot(t.x-current.x,t.y-current.y);if(d<range&&d<best){best=d;next=t}}if(!next)break;const chainDamage=effectiveDamage()*.65;next.health-=chainDamage;updateMirrorCloneThresholds(next,Math.max(0,next.health+chainDamage),Math.max(0,next.health));applyPoison(next);applyFreeze(next);applyStun(next);applyBurn(next,false,chainDamage);used.add(next);explosion(next.x,next.y);current=next;if(next.health<=0){if(enemies.includes(next))destroyEnemy(next);else destroyAsteroid(next)}}}


function spawnBlackHole(x,y){const hole={x,y,radius:24,life:150,maxLife:150,blackHole:true};window.activeBlackHoles=window.activeBlackHoles||[];window.activeBlackHoles.push(hole);}
function updateBlackHoles(){const holes=window.activeBlackHoles||[];for(const h of holes){h.life--;h.radius=Math.min(90,h.radius+1.2);[...asteroids,...enemies].forEach(t=>{if(t.health<=0)return;const dx=h.x-t.x,dy=h.y-t.y,d=Math.hypot(dx,dy)||1;const pull=Math.max(0,1-d/h.radius)*2.8;t.x+=dx/d*pull;t.y+=dy/d*pull;if(d<34){const blackHolePullDamage=effectiveDamage()*.18;t.health-=blackHolePullDamage;updateMirrorCloneThresholds(t,Math.max(0,t.health+blackHolePullDamage),Math.max(0,t.health));applyPoison(t);applyFreeze(t);applyStun(t);applyBurn(t,true,effectiveDamage()*.18);if(t.health<=0){if(enemies.includes(t))destroyEnemy(t);else destroyAsteroid(t)}}});if(h.life<=0){explosion(h.x,h.y);[...asteroids,...enemies].forEach(t=>{if(t.health<=0)return;const d=Math.hypot(t.x-h.x,t.y-h.y);if(d<110){const blackHoleDamage=effectiveDamage()*3*(1-d/110);t.health-=blackHoleDamage;updateMirrorCloneThresholds(t,Math.max(0,t.health+blackHoleDamage),Math.max(0,t.health));applyPoison(t);applyFreeze(t);applyStun(t);applyBurn(t,true,blackHoleDamage);if(t.health<=0){if(enemies.includes(t))destroyEnemy(t);else destroyAsteroid(t)}}})}}window.activeBlackHoles=holes.filter(h=>h.life>0)}
function drawBlackHoles(){(window.activeBlackHoles||[]).forEach(h=>{ctx.save();ctx.translate(h.x,h.y);const pulse=1+Math.sin(h.life*.15)*.08;ctx.scale(pulse,pulse);ctx.shadowColor="#7e57c2";ctx.shadowBlur=28;ctx.fillStyle="#050008";ctx.beginPath();ctx.arc(0,0,h.radius*.55,0,Math.PI*2);ctx.fill();ctx.strokeStyle="#b388ff";ctx.lineWidth=4;ctx.beginPath();ctx.arc(0,0,h.radius*.8,0,Math.PI*2);ctx.stroke();ctx.strokeStyle="#fff";ctx.globalAlpha=.65;ctx.lineWidth=2;ctx.beginPath();ctx.arc(0,0,h.radius,0,Math.PI*2);ctx.stroke();ctx.restore()})}
function rpgExplosion(x,y,isMini=false){const explosionRadius=isMini?120:120;explosion(x,y);screenShake(4,120);[...asteroids,...enemies].forEach(target=>{if(target.health<=0)return;const d=Math.hypot(target.x-x,target.y-y);if(d<=explosionRadius){const damage=effectiveDamage()*8*(1-d/explosionRadius*.5);target.health-=damage;updateMirrorCloneThresholds(target,Math.max(0,target.health+damage),Math.max(0,target.health));applyPoison(target);applyFreeze(target);applyStun(target);applyBurn(target,false,damage);if(target.health<=0){if(enemies.includes(target))destroyEnemy(target);else destroyAsteroid(target)}}});showMessage("💥 RPG התפוצץ!") }
function spawnAsteroid(){
  if(activeSpecialBossArea==="teleport"||activeSpecialBossArea==="gates")return;
  const size=Math.random()*18+12;
  const health=(10+size*.55+distance*.004)*5*difficultySettings[difficulty].enemyHP;
  const special=activeSpecialBossArea;
  let type="normal";
  if(special==="king"){type=["mirror","ice","gate","chaser","teleport","inversion","split"][Math.floor(Math.random()*7)];}
  else if(special==="mirror")type="mirror";else if(special==="ice")type="ice";else if(special==="gates")type="gate";else if(special==="chaser")type="chaser";else if(special==="teleport")type="teleport";else if(special==="inversion")type="inversion";else if(special==="doom")type="doom";
  const x=Math.random()*canvas.width,y=-50;let vx=0,vy=1,meteorSpeed=2+Math.random()*2+distance/1500,chaserMeteor=false;if(special==="chaser"){const dx=player.x-x,dy=player.y-y,len=Math.hypot(dx,dy)||1;vx=dx/len;vy=dy/len;meteorSpeed=18+distance/4000;chaserMeteor=true;}asteroids.push({x,y,size,health,maxHealth:health,speed:meteorSpeed,vx,vy,chaserMeteor,specialType:type,splitOnDeath:special==="split",teleportTimer:240,teleportMarker:null,teleportHidden:false,gateTimer:Math.random()*100,clone:false,cloneSpawned:false,splitDone:false,inversionReturning:false,shatterTimer:0});
}
function spawnEnemy(){
  if(activeSpecialBossArea==="teleport"||activeSpecialBossArea==="gates")return;
  const health=(100+distance*.012)*difficultySettings[difficulty].enemyHP;
  const special=activeSpecialBossArea;
  let type="normal";
  if(special==="king"){type=["mirror","ice","gate","chaser","teleport","inversion","split","doom"][Math.floor(Math.random()*8)];}
  else if(special==="mirror")type="mirror";else if(special==="ice")type="ice";else if(special==="gates")type="gate";else if(special==="chaser")type="chaser";else if(special==="teleport")type="teleport";else if(special==="inversion")type="inversion";else if(special==="doom")type="doom";
  const spawnHealth=special==="doom"?health*3:health;
  enemies.push({x:Math.random()*canvas.width,y:-50,health:spawnHealth,maxHealth:spawnHealth,speed:type==="chaser"?2.8:1.5+distance/3000,timer:type==="chaser"?999999:type==="doom"?70:100,boss:false,specialType:type,cloneSpawned:false,teleportTimer:240,teleportMarker:null,teleportHidden:false,gateTimer:Math.random()*120,splitOnDeath:special==="split",clone:false,splitDone:false});
}
function chooseSpecialBossType(){
  const pool=[
    ["mirror",20],
    ["ice",20],
    ["gates",15],
    ["chaser",15],
    ["teleport",10],
    ["inversion",10],
    ["split",5],
    ["king",3],
    ["doom",2]
  ];
  const total=pool.reduce((sum,item)=>sum+item[1],0);
  let roll=Math.random()*total;
  for(const [type,weight] of pool){roll-=weight;if(roll<0)return type}
  return "mirror";
}
function bossSpecialChanceAt(distanceValue){
  if(distanceValue<=1000)return 0;
  if(distanceValue<=3000)return .10;
  if(distanceValue<=5000)return .25;
  if(distanceValue<=7500)return .50;
  if(distanceValue<=10000)return .90;
  return 1;
}
function getBossDistanceList(){
  const list=[1000,2000,3000,4000,5000,6000,7500,9000,10000];
  if(distance>10000){
    const first=Math.max(12500,10000+2500);
    for(let d=first;d<=distance+5000;d+=2500)list.push(d);
  }else list.push(12500,15000,17500,20000);
  return [...new Set(list)].sort((a,b)=>a-b);
}
function planNextBoss(d){
  if(pendingBossPlan){
    if(pendingBossPlan.special && d>=pendingBossPlan.distance-pendingBossPlan.lead){
      setSpecialBossArea(pendingBossPlan.type,true);
    }
    return;
  }
  const all=getBossDistanceList();
  const bossDistance=all.find(v=>v>d+0.0001);
  if(!bossDistance||lastScheduledBossDistance===bossDistance)return;
  lastScheduledBossDistance=bossDistance;
  const special=Math.random()<bossSpecialChanceAt(bossDistance);
  const type=special?chooseSpecialBossType():"normal";
  const lead=bossDistance>=10000?2500:1000;
  pendingBossPlan={distance:bossDistance,special,type,lead};
  if(special&&d>=bossDistance-lead){
    setSpecialBossArea(type,true);
  }else if(!special){
    activeSpecialBossArea="";
    applyDistanceZone(false);
  }
}
function spawnBoss(type="normal"){
  const rawType=String(type),isSpecialRaw=rawType.startsWith("special:"),requestedSpecial=isSpecialRaw?rawType.slice(8):"";
  if(requestedSpecial==="teleport"){bossFightActive=false;pendingBossPlan=null;setSpecialBossArea("teleport",true);return;}
  if(requestedSpecial==="gates"){bossFightActive=false;pendingBossPlan=null;setSpecialBossArea("gates",true);return;}
  bossFightActive=true;
  const isBigSplit=rawType==="big"||requestedSpecial==="split",isSpecial=isSpecialRaw||isBigSplit,specialType=isBigSplit?"split":requestedSpecial;
  let health;if(isBigSplit)health=(700+distance*.5)*5*difficultySettings[difficulty].enemyHP;else if(isSpecial)health=(550+distance*.45)*5*difficultySettings[difficulty].enemyHP;else health=(350+distance*.35)*5*difficultySettings[difficulty].enemyHP;
  const side=Math.random()<.5?100:canvas.width-100;
  const boss={boss:true,bigBoss:isBigSplit,splitBoss:isBigSplit,specialBoss:isSpecial,specialType,x:side,y:120,startX:side,startY:120,health,maxHealth:health,direction:side<canvas.width/2?1:-1,timer:isBigSplit?999999:30,mirrorClone:false,cloneSpawned:false,specialTimer:0,phase:1,splitStage:isBigSplit?0:undefined,specialFireTimer:isBigSplit?600:0,inversionChargeTimer:specialType==="inversion"?300+Math.random()*300:0,inversionCooldownTimer:specialType==="inversion"?300+Math.random()*300:0,chaserReturning:false};
  enemies.push(boss);if(isSpecial)setSpecialBossArea(specialType,true);pendingBossPlan=null;showMessage(isBigSplit?"💥 בוס מתפצל הגיע!":isSpecial?"⚠️ בוס מיוחד הגיע!":"👹 בוס הגיע!");
}
function findNearestTarget(x,y){let nearest=null,best=Infinity;[...asteroids,...enemies].forEach(target=>{if(target.health<=0)return;const d=Math.hypot(target.x-x,target.y-y);if(d<best){best=d;nearest=target}});return nearest}
function getContinuousFireDamage(target,baseDamage,bullet,now){if(continuousFireLevel<=0||!target||!Number.isFinite(baseDamage)||baseDamage<0)return baseDamage;if(target.continuousFireLastBullet===bullet)return baseDamage;const lastDamage=Number(target.continuousFireLastDamage)||0,lastHitAt=Number(target.continuousFireLastHitAt)||0;const continuing=lastDamage>0&&now-lastHitAt<=1500;const rawDamage=continuing?lastDamage*(1+.2*continuousFireLevel):baseDamage;const damage=Math.round(rawDamage*1e10)/1e10;target.continuousFireLastDamage=damage;target.continuousFireLastHitAt=now;target.continuousFireLastBullet=bullet;return damage}
function applyPoison(target){
  if(poisonLevel<=0||!target||target.health<=0)return;
  const now=performance.now();
  const initialStrength=Math.min(1,Math.max(.1,poisonLevel*.1));
  const initialInterval=1000/Math.max(1,Math.min(9,poisonLevel));
  if(target.poisonStrength>0){
    target.poisonStrength+=((1-target.poisonStrength)*.1);
    target.poisonIntervalMs=Math.max(Number.EPSILON,(Number(target.poisonIntervalMs)||initialInterval)*.9);
  }else{
    target.poisonStrength=initialStrength;
    target.poisonIntervalMs=initialInterval;
    target.poisonNextTickAt=now+initialInterval;
  }
  target.poisonStrength=Math.min(1,target.poisonStrength);
  target.poisonIntervalMs=Math.max(Number.EPSILON,target.poisonIntervalMs);
}
function updatePoison(){
  const now=performance.now();
  const tickTarget=target=>{
    if(target.health<=1||!(Number(target.poisonStrength)>0))return;
    let interval=Math.max(Number.EPSILON,Number(target.poisonIntervalMs)||1000);
    if(!(Number(target.poisonNextTickAt)>0))target.poisonNextTickAt=now+interval;
    let ticks=0;
    while(now>=target.poisonNextTickAt&&target.health>1&&ticks<20){
      const isBoss=!!(target.boss&&enemies.includes(target));
      const strength=Math.min(1,Math.max(0,Number(target.poisonStrength)||0))*(isBoss ? 0.25 : 1);
      const poisonDamage=Math.min(Math.max(0,target.health-1),target.health*strength);
      if(poisonDamage>0){
        target.health-=poisonDamage;
        if(target.health<1)target.health=1;
        showDamageNumber(target,poisonDamage);
      }
      target.poisonNextTickAt+=interval;
      ticks++;
      interval=Math.max(Number.EPSILON,Number(target.poisonIntervalMs)||interval);
    }
  };
  asteroids.forEach(tickTarget);
  enemies.forEach(tickTarget);
}
function clearBurn(target){if(!target)return;target.burnUntil=0;target.burnBaseDamage=0;target.burnDamageTotal=0;target.burnDamagePerTick=0;target.burnDamageMultiplier=1;target.burnDurationMs=0;target.burnNextChance=0;target.burnHitCount=0;target.burnTicksRemaining=0;target.burnNextTickAt=0;}
function applyBurn(target,continuous=false,hitDamage=effectiveDamage()){
  if(burnLevel<=0||!target||target.health<=0)return false;
  const now=performance.now();
  if(continuous&&now<(Number(target.burnNextHitCheckAt)||0))return false;
  if(continuous)target.burnNextHitCheckAt=now+GalaxyBurn.tickIntervalMs;
  if(target.burnUntil>now){
    if(Math.random()>=target.burnNextChance){clearBurn(target);if(continuous)target.burnNextHitCheckAt=now+GalaxyBurn.tickIntervalMs;return false;}
    target.burnHitCount=(Number(target.burnHitCount)||1)+1;
    target.burnDamageMultiplier=GalaxyBurn.getNextDamageMultiplier(target.burnDamageMultiplier,burnLevel);
    target.burnDurationMs=GalaxyBurn.getNextDurationMs(target.burnDurationMs);
    target.burnNextChance=GalaxyBurn.getNextChance(target.burnNextChance,burnLevel);
    target.burnDamageTotal=target.burnBaseDamage*target.burnDamageMultiplier;
  }else{
    if(Math.random()>=GalaxyBurn.getChance(burnLevel))return false;
    const baseHitDamage=Math.max(0,Number(hitDamage)||0);
    target.burnBaseDamage=baseHitDamage*GalaxyBurn.getInitialDamageFraction(burnLevel);
    target.burnDamageMultiplier=1;target.burnDurationMs=GalaxyBurn.durationMs;
    target.burnNextChance=GalaxyBurn.getNextChance(GalaxyBurn.getChance(burnLevel),burnLevel);
    target.burnHitCount=1;target.burnDamageTotal=target.burnBaseDamage;
  }
  target.burnUntil=now+target.burnDurationMs;target.burnTicksRemaining=GalaxyBurn.ticksPerApplication;
  target.burnDamagePerTick=target.burnDamageTotal/GalaxyBurn.ticksPerApplication;
  target.burnNextTickAt=now+target.burnDurationMs/GalaxyBurn.ticksPerApplication;
  return true;
}
function updateBurn(){
  if(burnLevel<=0)return;
  const now=performance.now();
  const tickTarget=(target,destroyFn)=>{
    if(target.health<=0||!(target.burnUntil>0))return;
    while(target.burnTicksRemaining>0&&now>=target.burnNextTickAt&&target.burnNextTickAt<=target.burnUntil+1){
      const isBoss=!!(target.boss&&enemies.includes(target));
      const rawBurnDamage=target.burnDamagePerTick,burnDamage=isBoss?rawBurnDamage/4:rawBurnDamage;
      target.health-=burnDamage;showDamageNumber(target,burnDamage);target.burnTicksRemaining--;
      target.burnNextTickAt+=target.burnDurationMs/GalaxyBurn.ticksPerApplication;
      if(target.health<=0){clearBurn(target);destroyFn(target);return}
    }
    if(target.burnTicksRemaining<=0||now>=target.burnUntil)clearBurn(target);
  };
  asteroids.forEach(a=>tickTarget(a,destroyAsteroid));enemies.forEach(enemy=>tickTarget(enemy,destroyEnemy));
}
function drawBurnEffect(target,size,isSquare){
  if(!(target.burnUntil>performance.now()))return;
  const t=performance.now()*.001;
  ctx.save();
  for(let i=0;i<16;i++){
    const side=i%4,along=(((Math.floor(i/4)+.5)/4)*2-1)*size*.88;
    let bx,by,dx,dy,tx,ty;
    if(isSquare){
      if(side===0){bx=target.x+along;by=target.y-size;dx=0;dy=-1;tx=1;ty=0}
      else if(side===1){bx=target.x+size;by=target.y+along;dx=1;dy=0;tx=0;ty=1}
      else if(side===2){bx=target.x+along;by=target.y+size;dx=0;dy=1;tx=1;ty=0}
      else{bx=target.x-size;by=target.y+along;dx=-1;dy=0;tx=0;ty=1}
    }else{
      const angle=(i/16)*Math.PI*2;
      dx=Math.cos(angle);dy=Math.sin(angle);tx=-dy;ty=dx;
      bx=target.x+dx*size;by=target.y+dy*size;
    }
    const pulse=.75+.25*Math.sin(t*23+i*2.17);
    const len=(8+size*.35)*pulse,width=2.5+2*pulse;
    const tipX=bx+dx*len+tx*Math.sin(t*17+i)*2;
    const tipY=by+dy*len+ty*Math.sin(t*17+i)*2;
    const grad=ctx.createLinearGradient(bx,by,tipX,tipY);
    grad.addColorStop(0,"#ff2500");grad.addColorStop(.55,"#ff7600");grad.addColorStop(1,"#ffe66a");
    ctx.fillStyle=grad;
    ctx.beginPath();
    ctx.moveTo(bx-tx*width,by-ty*width);
    ctx.quadraticCurveTo(bx+dx*len*.3-tx*width*.7,by+dy*len*.3-ty*width*.7,tipX,tipY);
    ctx.quadraticCurveTo(bx+dx*len*.25+tx*width*.7,by+dy*len*.25+ty*width*.7,bx+tx*width,by+ty*width);
    ctx.closePath();ctx.fill();
  }
  for(let i=0;i<7;i++){
    const a=t*2.4+i*2.399,dist=size+5+((i*7)%11);
    const sx=target.x+Math.cos(a)*dist,sy=target.y+Math.sin(a)*dist;
    ctx.globalAlpha=.45+.55*(.5+.5*Math.sin(t*14+i*3));
    ctx.fillStyle=i%2?"#ff6818":"#fff0a0";
    ctx.shadowColor="#ff6b19";ctx.shadowBlur=7;
    ctx.fillRect(sx,sy,2,2);
  }
  ctx.restore();
}
function getFreezeChance(){return Math.min(1,.1*Math.pow(1.5,Math.max(0,freezeLevel-1)))}
function getFreezeStrength(){return Math.min(.9,.25*Math.pow(1.5,Math.max(0,freezeLevel-1)))}
function getFreezeDuration(){return 4000*Math.pow(1.33,Math.max(0,freezeLevel-1))}
function getEnemyFreezeFactor(enemy){if(!enemy||freezeLevel<=0||!(enemy.freezeUntil>performance.now()))return 1;return Math.max(.1,1-(Number(enemy.freezeStrength)||0))}
function applyFreeze(enemy){if(freezeLevel<=0||!enemy||enemy.health<=0)return false;if(Math.random()>=getFreezeChance())return false;const now=performance.now();enemy.freezeUntil=now+getFreezeDuration();enemy.freezeStrength=getFreezeStrength();return true}
function getStunChance(){return Math.min(1,.05*Math.pow(1.5,Math.max(0,stunLevel-1)))}
function getStunDuration(){return 1000*Math.pow(1.5,Math.max(0,stunLevel-1))}
function isStunnedEnemy(enemy){return !!(enemy&&enemy.health>0&&enemy.stunnedUntil>performance.now())}
function applyStun(enemy){if(stunLevel<=0||!enemy||enemy.health<=0||isStunnedEnemy(enemy))return false;if(Math.random()>=getStunChance())return false;const now=performance.now();enemy.stunnedUntil=now+getStunDuration();enemy.stunCenterX=enemy.x;enemy.stunCenterY=enemy.y;enemy.stunAngle=0;return true}
function updateStunnedEnemy(enemy){if(!isStunnedEnemy(enemy))return false;const now=performance.now();if(now>=enemy.stunnedUntil){enemy.stunnedUntil=0;enemy.x=enemy.stunCenterX;enemy.y=enemy.stunCenterY;return false}const radius=Math.max(.1,(Number(enemy.size)||22)*.15);enemy.stunAngle=(Number(enemy.stunAngle)||0)+(Math.PI*2/.2)/60;enemy.x=enemy.stunCenterX+Math.cos(enemy.stunAngle)*radius;enemy.y=enemy.stunCenterY+Math.sin(enemy.stunAngle)*radius;return true}
function getConfusionChance(){return Math.min(1,.05*Math.pow(1.5,Math.max(0,confusionLevel-1)))}
function getConfusionDuration(){return 4000*Math.pow(1.33,Math.max(0,confusionLevel-1))}
function getConfusionDamageMultiplier(){if(confusionLevel<=0)return 0;if(confusionLevel===1)return .125;return .25*Math.pow(1.5,Math.max(0,confusionLevel-2))}
function isConfusedEnemy(enemy){return !!(enemy&&!enemy.boss&&enemy.health>0&&enemy.confusedUntil>performance.now())}
function findConfusionTarget(source){let nearest=null,best=Infinity;const now=performance.now();for(const target of enemies){if(target===source||target.health<=0)continue;if(target.confusedUntil>now)continue;const d=Math.hypot(target.x-source.x,target.y-source.y);if(d<best){best=d;nearest=target}}return nearest}
function applyConfusion(enemy){if(confusionLevel<=0||!enemy||enemy.health<=0||enemy.boss)return false;if(Math.random()>=getConfusionChance())return false;const now=performance.now();enemy.confusedUntil=now+getConfusionDuration();enemy.confusedDamageMultiplier=getConfusionDamageMultiplier();enemy.confusedFireTimer=0;return true}
function updateConfusedEnemy(enemy){if(!isConfusedEnemy(enemy))return false;const worldMultiplier=worldSpeedMultiplier(),freezeFactor=getEnemyFreezeFactor(enemy),moveSpeed=Math.max(.1,Number(enemy.speed)||1)*worldMultiplier*freezeFactor;enemy.y-=moveSpeed;enemy.x-=Math.sin(Date.now()/500)*.7*worldMultiplier*freezeFactor;enemy.confusedFireTimer=(enemy.confusedFireTimer??0)-freezeFactor;if(enemy.confusedFireTimer<=0){const target=findConfusionTarget(enemy);if(target){const dx=target.x-enemy.x,dy=target.y-enemy.y,len=Math.hypot(dx,dy)||1;enemyBullets.push({x:enemy.x,y:enemy.y,vx:dx/len*3.5,vy:dy/len*3.5,confusedBullet:true,confusedSource:enemy,confusedDamage:Number(enemy.confusedDamageMultiplier)||getConfusionDamageMultiplier(),freezeSpeedMultiplier:freezeFactor});enemy.confusedFireTimer=Math.max(25,100-(Math.max(0,confusionLevel-1)*5))}else enemy.confusedFireTimer=20}return true}
function addXP(amount){amount*=difficultySettings[difficulty].xp;amount*=1+xpBoostLevel*.25;xp+=amount;if(ultraUnlocked){ultraPoints=Math.min(ultraPointsCap,ultraPoints+amount);runUltraPointsEarned+=amount;}while(xp>=xpNeeded){xp-=xpNeeded;level++;if(ultraUnlocked)ultraPointsCap+=500;const ach=getAchievementStats();ach.levels++;saveAchievementStats(ach);checkAchievements();xpNeeded=Math.floor(xpNeeded*1.25);addPlayerXP(10,false,"עליית רמה");if(rpgLevel>0)ammo.rpg+=5;if(shotgunLevel>0)ammo.shotgun+=15;openLevelUp()}updateHUD()}
const numberedUpgradeIds=new Set(["damage","fireRate","maxLives","speed","extraFire","splitBullet","xpBoost","luck","dodge","piercing","sword","levelHeal","chainLightning","lowHealthRage","poison","burn","freeze","stun","confusion","continuousFire","expandedChoice","flamethrower","laserHeat","chainExplosion","deadlyLoot","infernoBomb"]);
function getUpgradeLevel(id){
  switch(id){
    case"damage":return damageLevel;
    case"fireRate":return fireRateLevel;
    case"maxLives":return maxLivesLevel;
    case"speed":return speedLevel;
    case"extraFire":return extraFireLevel;
    case"xpBoost":return xpBoostLevel;
    case"luck":return luckLevel;
    case"dodge":return Math.round(dodgeChance/.08);
    case"piercing":return piercingLevel;
    case"sword":return swordCount;
    case"levelHeal":return levelHealLevel;
    case"homing":return homingLevel;
    case"chainLightning":return chainLightningLevel;
    case"extraRow":return extraRowLevel;
    case"lowHealthRage":return lowHealthBoostLevel;
    case"splitBullet":return splitBulletLevel;
    case"poison":return poisonLevel;
    case"burn":return burnLevel;
    case"freeze":return freezeLevel;
    case"stun":return stunLevel;
    case"confusion":return confusionLevel;
    case"continuousFire":return continuousFireLevel;
    case"expandedChoice":return expandedChoiceLevel;
    case"chainExplosion":return chainExplosionLevel;
    case"deadlyLoot":return deadlyLootLevel;
    case"infernoBomb":return infernoBombLevel;
    case"laserHeat":return laserHeatLevel;
    default:return 0;
  }
}
function toRomanNumeral(value){
  const map=[[10,"X"],[9,"IX"],[5,"V"],[4,"IV"],[1,"I"]];
  let result="";
  for(const [n,symbol] of map){while(value>=n){result+=symbol;value-=n}}
  return result||"I";
}
function getUpgradeCardTitle(upgrade){
  if(!numberedUpgradeIds.has(upgrade.id))return upgrade.title;
  const level=getUpgradeLevel(upgrade.id);
  return level<=0?upgrade.title:upgrade.title+" "+toRomanNumeral(level+1);
}
