finish()});upgradeContainer.appendChild(card)});playLevelUpAnimation()}
function applyUpgrade(u){const livesBefore=player.lives;switch(u.id){case"infernoBomb":infernoBombLevel=Math.min(9,infernoBombLevel+1);if(infernoBombLevel===1){infernoBombMaxAmmo=4;infernoBombAmmo=4}else{infernoBombMaxAmmo=Math.min(12,infernoBombMaxAmmo+1);infernoBombAmmo=Math.min(infernoBombMaxAmmo,infernoBombAmmo+1)}showMessage("💣 פצצת תופת "+toRomanNumeral(infernoBombLevel)+" · "+infernoBombAmmo+"/"+infernoBombMaxAmmo);break;case"deadlyLoot":deadlyLootLevel=Math.min(9,deadlyLootLevel+1);showMessage("🎁 שלל קטלני "+toRomanNumeral(deadlyLootLevel));break;case"chainExplosion":chainExplosionLevel=Math.min(window.GalaxyChainExplosion.maxLevel,chainExplosionLevel+1);{const stats=window.GalaxyChainExplosion.getStats(chainExplosionLevel);u.rarity=stats.rarity;u.rarityClass=stats.rarityClass;}showMessage("💥 פיצוץ שרשרת "+window.GalaxyChainExplosion.getStats(chainExplosionLevel).roman+" נפתח!");break;case"ultraMode":ultraUnlocked=true;ultraModeActive=false;ultraPoints=0;ultraPointsCap=5000;ultraSpentForShield=0;ultraShields=0;ultraCooldownUntil=0;ultraLastCountdown=-1;showMessage("🌈 מצב אולטרה נפתח!");break;case"ultraLaser":if(ultraUnlocked){ultraLaserUnlocked=true;ultraLaserActive=false;ultraLaserCooldownUntil=0;ultraLaserElapsed=0;ultraLaserPointsSpent=0;ultraLaserFadeUntil=0;showMessage("🌈 אולטרה לייזר נפתח!")}break;case"damage":damageLevel++;player.damage+=4;swordDamage+=2;break;case"fireRate":fireRateLevel++;player.fireRate=Math.max(3,player.fireRate-2);break;case"maxLives":maxLivesLevel++;player.maxLives++;player.lives++;break;case"healOne":if(!healOneUsed){healOneUsed=true;player.lives=Math.min(player.maxLives,player.lives+1)}break;case"speed":speedLevel++;player.speed+=.7;break;case"extraFire":extraFireLevel++;break;case"xpBoost":xpBoostLevel++;break;case"luck":luckLevel++;break;case"dodge":dodgeChance=Math.min(.5,dodgeChance+.08);break;case"fullHeal":if(!fullHealUsed){fullHealUsed=true;player.lives=player.maxLives}break;case"extraDrone":if(maxDrones<5){maxDrones++;extraDroneLevel++;showMessage("🛸 מקסימום מיני חלליות: "+maxDrones)}break;case"drone":if(drones.length<maxDrones)drones.push({type:"normal",angle:Math.random()*Math.PI*2,radius:70,timer:20,rotationSpeed:.025});break;case"rpg":rpgLevel++;ammo.rpg+=10;break;case"piercing":piercingLevel++;break;case"sword":swordCount++;break;case"revive":reviveAvailable=true;break;case"fillShields":ultraShields=player.maxLives;showMessage("🛡️ כל המגנים מולאו!");updateHUD();break;case"levelHeal":levelHealLevel++;{const healAmount=Math.min(levelHealLevel,Math.max(0,player.maxLives-player.lives));if(healAmount>0){player.lives+=healAmount;triggerHealingEffect(healAmount);}}break;case"shotgun":shotgunLevel++;ammo.shotgun+=30;break;case"droneUpgrade":if(drones.length===0)drones.push({type:"upgraded",angle:Math.random()*Math.PI*2,radius:85,timer:10,rotationSpeed:.05});else drones.forEach(d=>{if(d.type==="normal"){d.type="upgraded";d.radius=85;d.rotationSpeed=.05}});droneTier=Math.max(droneTier,1);break;case"homing":homingLevel++;break;case"chainLightning":chainLightningLevel++;break;case"rpgMiniShip":amazingDroneLevel++;if(!drones.some(d=>d.type==="rpgMini")){drones.push({type:"rpgMini",angle:Math.random()*Math.PI*2,radius:115,timer:12,rotationSpeed:.035})}break;case"blackHole":blackHoleMaxAmmo+=4;blackHoleAmmo+=4;ammo.blackHole=blackHoleAmmo;showMessage("🌌 +4 יריות חור שחור!");break;case"extraRow":extraRowLevel++;break;case"laser":laserMode=true;break;case"laserHeat":laserHeatLevel=Math.min(5,laserHeatLevel+1);showMessage("🔥 לייזר מתחמם "+toRomanNumeral(laserHeatLevel));break;case"flamethrower":flamethrowerMode=true;flamethrowerLevel=Math.min(10,flamethrowerLevel+1);break;case"lowHealthRage":lowHealthBoostLevel++;break;case"splitBullet":splitBulletLevel++;showMessage("🔱 קליע מפוצל ×"+splitBulletLevel);break;case"poison":poisonLevel=Math.min(9,poisonLevel+1);showMessage("☠️ רעל "+toRomanNumeral(poisonLevel));break;case"burn":burnLevel=Math.min(GalaxyBurn.maxLevel,burnLevel+1);showMessage("🔥 צריבה "+toRomanNumeral(burnLevel));break;case"freeze":freezeLevel++;showMessage("🧊 הקפאה ×"+freezeLevel);break;case"stun":stunLevel++;showMessage("⚡ שיתוק ×"+stunLevel);break;case"confusion":confusionLevel++;showMessage("🌀 בלבול ×"+confusionLevel);break;case"continuousFire":continuousFireLevel++;showMessage("🎯 ירי רציף ×"+continuousFireLevel);break;case"expandedChoice":expandedChoiceLevel=Math.min(3,expandedChoiceLevel+1);showMessage("🃏 בחירה מורחבת "+toRomanNumeral(expandedChoiceLevel));break;case"droneMega":if(drones.length===0)drones.push({type:"large",angle:Math.random()*Math.PI*2,radius:100,timer:10,rotationSpeed:.075});else drones.forEach(d=>{d.type="large";d.radius=100;d.rotationSpeed=.075});droneTier=2;break}if(player.lives>livesBefore&&u.id!=="maxLives")triggerHealingEffect(player.lives-livesBefore);updateHUD()}
function updateUltraLaser(){
  if(!ultraLaserActive||!gameRunning)return;
  const now=performance.now(),elapsed=Math.min(10,ultraLaserElapsed+1/60),dt=elapsed-ultraLaserElapsed;
  ultraLaserElapsed=elapsed;
  const targetSpent=Math.min(5000,elapsed*500),spend=Math.max(0,targetSpent-ultraLaserPointsSpent);
  ultraLaserPointsSpent=targetSpent;
  ultraPoints=Math.max(0,ultraPoints-spend);
  if(dt>0){
    const dx=mouse.x-player.x,dy=mouse.y-player.y,len=Math.hypot(dx,dy)||1,dirX=dx/len,dirY=dy/len,range=1400;
    const heatStats=laserHeatLevel>0?getLaserHeatStats(laserHeatLevel):{startingDamageMultiplier:1,spatialDamageMultiplier:1};
    const startDps=getBaseLaserDps()*heatStats.startingDamageMultiplier,maxDps=getBaseLaserDps()*heatStats.spatialDamageMultiplier,baseDps=startDps+(maxDps-startDps)*(laserHeatLevel>0?laserHeat:0);
    const damage=baseDps*(ultraModeActive?5:2)*dt;
     enemies.forEach(target=>{if(target.health>0){const ex=target.x-player.x,ey=target.y-player.y,len=Math.hypot(mouse.x-player.x,mouse.y-player.y)||1,forward=(ex*(mouse.x-player.x)+ey*(mouse.y-player.y))/len,side=Math.abs(ex*(mouse.y-player.y)-ey*(mouse.x-player.x))/len;if(forward>0&&forward<1400&&side<Math.max(10,player.size*.45))target.ultraLaserHit=true;}});
    [...enemies,...asteroids].forEach(target=>{
      if(target.health<=0)return;
      const ex=target.x-player.x,ey=target.y-player.y,forward=ex*dirX+ey*dirY,side=Math.abs(ex*dirY-ey*dirX);
      if(forward>0&&forward<range&&side<(ultraModeActive?105:35)){
        const isEnemy=enemies.includes(target),isBoss=isEnemy&&!!target.boss;
        if(ultraModeActive&&!isBoss){
          const push=Math.min(9,4+Math.max(0,105-side)*.08);
          target.x+=dirX*push;target.y+=dirY*push;
        }
        target.health-=damage;
        showDamageNumber(target,damage);
        if(target.health<=0){if(isEnemy)destroyEnemy(target);else destroyAsteroid(target)}
      }
    });
    if(ultraModeActive){
      // Only the empowered Ultra Laser deflects hostile projectiles.
      enemyBullets.forEach(b=>{
        if(b.dead)return;
        const bx=b.x-player.x,by=b.y-player.y,forward=bx*dirX+by*dirY,side=Math.abs(bx*dirY-by*dirX);
        if(forward>0&&forward<range&&side<105){
          const dot=b.vx*dirX+b.vy*dirY;
          b.vx-=2*dot*dirX;b.vy-=2*dot*dirY;b.deflectedByUltraLaser=true;
        }
      });
    }
  }
  if(elapsed>=10){ultraLaserActive=false;ultraLaserFadeUntil=now+500;ultraLaserCooldownUntil=now+20000;ultraLaserPointsSpent=5000;showMessage("🌈 אולטרה לייזר הסתיים — טעינה של 20 שניות");}
  updateUltraLaserButton();
}
function drawUltraLaser(){
  const now=performance.now();if(!ultraLaserActive&&now>=ultraLaserFadeUntil)return;
  const alpha=ultraLaserActive?1:Math.max(0,Math.min(1,(ultraLaserFadeUntil-now)/500));
  const dx=mouse.x-player.x,dy=mouse.y-player.y,len=Math.hypot(dx,dy)||1,endX=player.x+dx/len*1400,endY=player.y+dy/len*1400;
  const colors=["#ff1744","#ff9800","#ffeb3b","#35e06f","#19b7ff","#6a4cff","#e040fb"];
  const thickness=(12+laserHeat*3)*(ultraModeActive?3:1);
  ctx.save();ctx.globalAlpha=alpha;ctx.lineCap="round";colors.forEach((color,i)=>{ctx.strokeStyle=color;ctx.shadowColor=color;ctx.shadowBlur=8*(ultraModeActive?3:1);ctx.lineWidth=(thickness-i*(thickness/11))*1;ctx.beginPath();ctx.moveTo(player.x+dx/len*18,player.y+dy/len*18);ctx.lineTo(endX,endY);ctx.stroke()});ctx.restore();
}
function updateLaser(){
  const dt=1/60;
  if(!laserMode)return;
  let hitTarget=false;
  if(laserOn&&gameRunning){
    const dx=mouse.x-player.x,dy=mouse.y-player.y,len=Math.hypot(dx,dy)||1,dirX=dx/len,dirY=dy/len,maxRange=1200;
    [...enemies,...asteroids].forEach(target=>{if(target.health<=0)return;const ex=target.x-player.x,ey=target.y-player.y,forward=ex*dirX+ey*dirY,side=Math.abs(ex*dirY-ey*dirX);if(forward>0&&forward<maxRange&&side<35){if(enemies.includes(target))hitTarget=true;const heatStats=getLaserHeatStats(laserHeatLevel),baseDps=getBaseLaserDps(),startDps=baseDps*heatStats.startingDamageMultiplier,maxDps=baseDps*heatStats.spatialDamageMultiplier,dps=startDps+(maxDps-startDps)*laserHeat;const laserDamage=laserHeatLevel>0?dps*dt*(ultraModeActive?2:1):effectiveDamage()*.25;target.health-=laserDamage;showDamageNumber(target,laserDamage);applyPoison(target);applyFreeze(target);applyStun(target);applyBurn(target,true,laserDamage);if(hitEffectsEnabled&&laserHeatLevel>0)screenShake(.35+laserHeat*2.2,45);if(target.health<=0){if(enemies.includes(target))destroyEnemy(target);else destroyAsteroid(target)}}});
  }
  if(laserHeatLevel>0){
    const heatStats=getLaserHeatStats(laserHeatLevel);if(hitTarget){laserHeat=Math.min(1,laserHeat+dt/heatStats.heatTime);}
    else {laserHeat=Math.max(0,laserHeat-(2*Math.sqrt(Math.max(laserHeat,0))/heatStats.coolTime)*dt);}
  }else laserHeat=0;
}
function getLaserHeatColor(){return `hsl(${210-210*laserHeat},100%,60%)`;}
function applyFlamethrowerBurn(target,flameDamage){if(!target||target.health<=0||target.flameBurnUntil>performance.now())return false;const now=performance.now(),level=Math.max(1,flamethrowerLevel),burnPercent=.2+.1*(level-1);target.flameBurnUntil=now+2500;target.flameBurnNextTickAt=now+500;target.flameBurnTicksRemaining=5;target.flameBurnDamagePerTick=flameDamage*burnPercent/5;return true}
function updateFlamethrowerBurn(){if(flamethrowerLevel<=0)return;const now=performance.now();const tickTarget=(target,destroyFn)=>{if(target.health<=0||!(target.flameBurnUntil>now))return;while(target.flameBurnTicksRemaining>0&&now>=target.flameBurnNextTickAt){const burnDamage=target.flameBurnDamagePerTick;target.health-=burnDamage;showDamageNumber(target,burnDamage);target.flameBurnTicksRemaining--;target.flameBurnNextTickAt+=500;if(target.health<=0){destroyFn(target);return}}if(target.flameBurnTicksRemaining<=0)target.flameBurnUntil=0};asteroids.forEach(a=>tickTarget(a,destroyAsteroid));enemies.forEach(enemy=>tickTarget(enemy,destroyEnemy))}
function updateFlamethrower(){if(!flamethrowerOn||!flamethrowerMode||!gameRunning)return;const dx=mouse.x-player.x,dy=mouse.y-player.y,len=Math.hypot(dx,dy)||1,dirX=dx/len,dirY=dy/len,level=Math.max(1,flamethrowerLevel),scale=1+.5*(level-1),range=220*scale;[...enemies,...asteroids].forEach(target=>{if(target.health<=0)return;const ex=target.x-player.x,ey=target.y-player.y,forward=ex*dirX+ey*dirY,side=Math.abs(ex*dirY-ey*dirX);if(forward>0&&forward<range&&side<55){const flameDamage=effectiveDamage()*.18*scale;target.health-=flameDamage;showDamageNumber(target,flameDamage);applyFlamethrowerBurn(target,flameDamage);applyPoison(target);applyFreeze(target);applyStun(target);applyBurn(target,true,flameDamage);if(target.health<=0){if(enemies.includes(target))destroyEnemy(target);else destroyAsteroid(target)}}})}
function updateSwords(){if(swordCount<=0)return;swordAngle+=.015+fireRateLevel*.004;for(let i=0;i<swordCount;i++){const angle=swordAngle+i*Math.PI*2/swordCount,sx=player.x+Math.cos(angle)*swordRange,sy=player.y+Math.sin(angle)*swordRange;enemies.forEach(enemy=>{if(enemy.health<=0)return;if(Math.hypot(sx-enemy.x,sy-enemy.y)<35){const swordHitDamage=swordDamage*(rageActive()?1.25:1)*(ultraModeActive?2:1);enemy.health-=swordHitDamage;showDamageNumber(enemy,swordHitDamage);applyPoison(enemy);applyFreeze(enemy);applyBurn(enemy,true,swordHitDamage);if(enemy.health<=0)destroyEnemy(enemy)}});asteroids.forEach(a=>{if(a.health<=0)return;if(Math.hypot(sx-a.x,sy-a.y)<35){const swordHitDamage=swordDamage*(lowHealthActive()?1.25:1)*(deadlyLootRageActive()?1.25:1)*(ultraModeActive?2:1);a.health-=swordHitDamage;showDamageNumber(a,swordHitDamage);applyPoison(a);applyFreeze(a);applyBurn(a,true,swordHitDamage);if(a.health<=0)destroyAsteroid(a)}});enemyBullets.forEach(b=>{if(Math.hypot(sx-b.x,sy-b.y)<30)b.dead=true})}}
function updateDrones(){drones.forEach(drone=>{drone.angle+=drone.rotationSpeed;drone.x=player.x+Math.cos(drone.angle)*drone.radius;drone.y=player.y+Math.sin(drone.angle)*drone.radius;drone.timer--;if(drone.timer>0)return;const target=findNearestTarget(drone.x,drone.y);if(target){const dx=target.x-drone.x,dy=target.y-drone.y,baseAngle=Math.atan2(dy,dx);let count=1;if(drone.type==="upgraded")count=3;if(drone.type==="large")count=7;if(drone.type==="rpgMini")count=1;for(let i=0;i<count;i++){const spread=count===1?0:(i-(count-1)/2)*.13;createDroneBullet(drone,baseAngle+spread,drone.type==="rpgMini"?12:(drone.type==="large"?7+droneDamageLevel*4:5+droneDamageLevel*3),drone.type==="large"?6:drone.type==="rpgMini"?9:5)}}drone.timer=drone.type==="normal"?40:drone.type==="upgraded"?20:13})}
function createDroneBullet(drone,angle,damage,size){bullets.push({x:drone.x,y:drone.y,vx:Math.cos(angle)*7,vy:Math.sin(angle)*7,damage:damage*(ultraModeActive?2:1),size,droneBullet:true,rpg:drone.type==="rpgMini",rpgMini:drone.type==="rpgMini",homing:homingLevel>0,piercing:piercingLevel>0,piercingEnabled:piercingLevel>0,targetHitCounts:[]})}
function damagePlayer(){
  if(invincible||ultraModeActive||!gameRunning)return false;
  if(ultraShields>0){ultraShields--;shieldBubbleFlashUntil=performance.now()+2500;invincible=true;invincibleTimer=90;showMessage("🛡️ המגן חסם פגיעה!");updateHUD();return true;}
  if(Math.random()<dodgeChance){
    dodgeGhostTimer=60;
    showMessage("🌀 התחמקת מהפגיעה!");
    return false;
  }
  player.lives--;runLivesLost=true;
  triggerHitFlash();
  screenShake(4,120);
  resetTemporarySpeedCombo();
  invincible=true;
  invincibleTimer=300;
  if(player.lives<=0){
    if(reviveAvailable){
      reviveAvailable=false;
      player.lives=player.maxLives;
      triggerHealingEffect(player.lives);
      invincibleTimer=420;
      showMessage("💖 ניצלת וחזרת לחיים עם חיים מלאים!");
    }else gameOver();
  }else showMessage("💥 נפגעת! ❤️ "+player.lives);
  updateHUD();
  return true;
}

function deadlyLootEligibility(){return{life:player.lives<player.maxLives,shield:ultraUnlocked&&ultraShields<player.maxLives,xp:true,ammo:rpgLevel>0||shotgunLevel>0,rage:!deadlyLootRageActive()&&!lowHealthActive()}}
function spawnDeadlyLootDrops(entity){if(deadlyLootLevel<=0)return;const drops=window.GalaxyDeadlyLoot.rollDrops(entity,deadlyLootLevel,luckLevel,deadlyLootEligibility());for(const drop of drops){const angle=(Math.random()-.5)*.7;deadlyLootPickups.push({...drop,x:entity.x+(Math.random()-.5)*24,y:entity.y,vx:Math.sin(angle)*.8,vy:1.45+Math.random()*.65,drift:Math.random()*Math.PI*2,life:900,speedMultiplier:drop.doubled?1.5:1})}}
function applyDeadlyLootPickup(drop){const amount=drop.amount;const livesBefore=player.lives;spawnFloatingReward(drop);switch(drop.kind){case"life":player.lives=Math.min(player.maxLives,player.lives+amount);if(player.lives>livesBefore)triggerHealingEffect(player.lives-livesBefore);break;case"shield":ultraShields=Math.min(player.maxLives,ultraShields+amount);break;case"xp":addXP(amount);break;case"ammo":ammo.shotgun+=amount.shotgun;ammo.rpg+=amount.rpg;if(infernoBombLevel>0&&infernoBombAmmo<infernoBombMaxAmmo&&Math.random()<infernoBombAmmoChance()/100){infernoBombAmmo=Math.min(infernoBombMaxAmmo,infernoBombAmmo+1);showMessage("💣 +1 פצצה!")}break;case"rage":deadlyLootRageUntil=Math.max(performance.now(),deadlyLootRageUntil)+(Number(amount)||0)*1000;break}const messages={life:"❤️ +"+amount,shield:"🛡️ +"+amount,xp:"⭐ +"+Number(amount).toLocaleString("en-US")+" XP",ammo:"🔫 תחמושת נאספה",rage:"🔥 מצב זעם הופעל!"};showMessage(messages[drop.kind]||"🎁 נאסף שלל!");updateHUD()}
function updateDeadlyLootPickups(){for(let i=deadlyLootPickups.length-1;i>=0;i--){const p=deadlyLootPickups[i];p.drift=(Number(p.drift)||0)+.055;const speed=Number(p.speedMultiplier)||1;p.x+=((Number(p.vx)||0)+Math.sin(p.drift)*.35)*speed;p.y+=(Number(p.vy)||1.5)*speed;p.life=(Number(p.life)||900)-1;if(p.x<12||p.x>canvas.width-12)p.vx*=-1;if(Math.hypot(p.x-player.x,p.y-player.y)<player.size+14){applyDeadlyLootPickup(p);deadlyLootPickups.splice(i,1)}else if(p.y>canvas.height+30||p.life<=0)deadlyLootPickups.splice(i,1)}}
function drawDeadlyLootPickups(){const icons={life:"❤️",shield:"🛡️",xp:"⭐",ammo:"🔫",rage:"🔥"};deadlyLootPickups.forEach(p=>{ctx.save();ctx.globalAlpha=.95;ctx.fillStyle=p.doubled?"rgba(255,190,40,.24)":"rgba(255,255,255,.16)";ctx.strokeStyle=p.doubled?"#ffd54f":"#ffffff";ctx.lineWidth=p.doubled?3:2;ctx.shadowColor=p.doubled?"#ffb300":"#4cc9f0";ctx.shadowBlur=p.doubled?18:10;ctx.beginPath();ctx.arc(p.x,p.y,15,0,Math.PI*2);ctx.fill();ctx.stroke();ctx.font="19px Arial";ctx.textAlign="center";ctx.textBaseline="middle";ctx.fillText(icons[p.kind]||"🎁",p.x,p.y);if(p.doubled){ctx.font="bold 10px Arial";ctx.fillStyle="#fff";ctx.fillText("×2",p.x+12,p.y-13)}ctx.restore()})}

function destroyAsteroid(a){
  if(!a || a.health>0)return;
  spawnMeteoriteFragments(a);
  spawnDeadlyLootDrops({kind:"meteorite",size:a.size,x:a.x,y:a.y});
  a.health=0;
  if(chainExplosionLevel>0){
    const chainId=a.chainReactionId||(++chainExplosionSequence);
    if(a.chainNoChain)triggerChainExplosion(a,chainId,false,true);
    else triggerChainExplosion(a,chainId,true,false);
  }
  const ach=getAchievementStats();
  ach.meteors++;
  saveAchievementStats(ach);
  checkAchievements();
  const wasNearPlayer=Math.hypot(a.x-player.x,a.y-player.y)<=(a.size+70);
  explosion(a.x,a.y);
  unlockCollectionItem(collectionIdForEntity(a,true));
  if(a.specialType==="mirror"&&!a.clone){const savedHealth=a.health;a.health=0;updateMirrorCloneThresholds(a);a.health=savedHealth;}else if(a.splitOnDeath&&!a.clone){
    splitEntityIntoClones(a);
  }
  if(a.specialType==="ice"&&wasNearPlayer){
    freezeTimer=Math.max(freezeTimer,420);
  }
  let reward=5;
  if(a.size<18)reward=2;
  else if(a.size<24)reward=3;
  addPlayerXP(reward,true,a.size<18?"מטאוריט קטן":a.size<24?"מטאוריט בינוני":"מטאוריט גדול");
  addXP(12+Math.floor(a.size*.8)+Math.floor(distance*.01));
}

function updateMirrorCloneThresholds(entity,previousHealth=entity.health,currentHealth=entity.health){
  if(!entity||entity.clone||entity.mirrorClone||entity.specialType!=="mirror")return;
  const maxHealth=Math.max(1,Number(entity.maxHealth)||1),before=Math.max(0,Number(previousHealth)||0),after=Math.max(0,Number(currentHealth)||0),thresholds=entity.boss?[.75,.5,.25]:[.75,.25];
  entity.mirrorCloneThresholds=entity.mirrorCloneThresholds||{};
  thresholds.forEach(threshold=>{const key=String(Math.round(threshold*100)),thresholdHealth=maxHealth*threshold;if(before>thresholdHealth&&after<=thresholdHealth&&!entity.mirrorCloneThresholds[key]){entity.mirrorCloneThresholds[key]=true;spawnMirrorClonePair(entity);}});
}
function spawnMirrorClonePair(entity){
  if(!entity)return;
  const cloneHealth=Math.max(1,(Number(entity.maxHealth)||1)*.25);
  if(entity.boss){
    const forward=(Number(entity.direction)||1)*45;
    for(let i=0;i<2;i++)enemies.push({boss:true,bigBoss:false,splitBoss:false,specialBoss:true,specialType:"mirror",x:entity.x+forward,y:entity.y+(i===0?-24:24),health:cloneHealth,maxHealth:cloneHealth,direction:entity.direction,timer:60,mirrorClone:true,clone:true,cloneSpawned:true,mirrorOriginal:entity,teleportTimer:0,gateTimer:0,phase:1,specialTimer:0});
  }else if(enemies.includes(entity)){
    for(let i=0;i<2;i++)enemies.push({boss:false,x:entity.x+(i===0?-35:35),y:entity.y,health:cloneHealth,maxHealth:cloneHealth,speed:Math.max(.5,(Number(entity.speed)||1)*.5),timer:200,specialType:"mirror",mirrorClone:true,clone:true,cloneSpawned:true,teleportTimer:240,teleportMarker:null,teleportHidden:false,gateTimer:180,splitDone:true});
  }else{
    for(let i=0;i<2;i++)asteroids.push({x:entity.x+(i===0?-35:35),y:entity.y,size:entity.size,health:cloneHealth,maxHealth:cloneHealth,speed:Math.max(.5,(Number(entity.speed)||1)*.5),specialType:"mirror",mirrorClone:true,clone:true,cloneSpawned:true,splitDone:true,splitOnDeath:false,teleportTimer:240,teleportMarker:null,teleportHidden:false,gateTimer:180,inversionReturning:false,shatterTimer:0});
  }
}
function splitEntityIntoClones(entity){
  if(entity.splitDone)return;
  entity.splitDone=true;
  const isEnemy=enemies.includes(entity),childHealth=entity.maxHealth/2,offset=70;
  if(isEnemy){
    for(let i=0;i<2;i++)enemies.push({boss:false,x:entity.x+(i===0?-offset:offset),y:entity.y,health:childHealth,maxHealth:childHealth,speed:Math.max(1,entity.speed*.95),timer:entity.specialType==="chaser"?999999:100,specialType:entity.specialType,clone:true,cloneSplit:true,splitClone:true,splitDone:true,cloneSpawned:true,teleportTimer:240,teleportMarker:null,teleportHidden:false,gateTimer:180,inversionReturning:false,shatterTimer:0});
  }else{
    for(let i=0;i<2;i++)asteroids.push({x:entity.x+(i===0?-offset:offset),y:entity.y,size:Math.max(10,entity.size*.88),health:childHealth,maxHealth:childHealth,speed:Math.max(1,entity.speed*.95),specialType:entity.specialType,splitClone:true,splitDone:true,clone:true,splitOnDeath:false,cloneSpawned:true,teleportTimer:240,teleportMarker:null,teleportHidden:false,gateTimer:180,inversionReturning:false,shatterTimer:0});
  }
}
function triggerChainExplosion(enemy,chainId,allowDamage,suppressChains){
  if(!enemy||enemy.chainExplosionTriggered||chainExplosionLevel<=0)return;
  enemy.chainExplosionTriggered=true;
  const stats=window.GalaxyChainExplosion.getStats(chainExplosionLevel),boss=!!enemy.boss;
  const sourceSize=Math.max(12,Number(enemy.size)||(boss?60:25));
  const radius=sourceSize*(boss?3.8:2.2)*stats.sizeMultiplier;
  const depth=Math.max(1,Number(enemy.chainDepth)||1);
  chainExplosionEffects.push({x:enemy.x,y:enemy.y,boss,life:boss?38:26,maxLife:boss?38:26,maxRadius:radius,depth});
  screenShake(boss?14:Math.min(9,2.5+radius/45),boss?300:130+Math.min(100,radius));
  if(meteoriteBreakEffectEnabled){
    const pixelColor=enemy.color||enemy.visualColor||(boss?"#b85cff":"#ff8a36");
    const count=Math.min(180,Math.round((boss?52:18)*(sourceSize/25)*Math.sqrt(stats.sizeMultiplier)));
    for(let i=0;i<count;i++){
      const angle=Math.random()*Math.PI*2,speed=(1.8+Math.random()*(boss?7:4))*Math.sqrt(stats.sizeMultiplier);
      const size=Math.max(2,Math.min(16,(2+Math.random()*(boss?6:4))*(sourceSize/25)));
      meteoriteFragments.push({x:enemy.x,y:enemy.y,vx:Math.cos(angle)*speed,vy:Math.sin(angle)*speed-1.1,rotation:Math.random()*Math.PI*2,spin:(Math.random()-.5)*.4,size,life:boss?55+Math.floor(Math.random()*25):32+Math.floor(Math.random()*22),maxLife:80,shade:120,shape:[1,1,1,1],particleType:boss?"boss":"enemy",color:pixelColor});
    }
  }
  if(!allowDamage)return;
  const damage=Math.max(0,Number(enemy.maxHealth)||Number(enemy.health)||0)*stats.damagePercent/100;
  const targets=[...enemies,...asteroids];
  for(const target of targets){
    if(target===enemy||target.health<=0||target.lastChainExplosionId===chainId)continue;
    if(Math.hypot(target.x-enemy.x,target.y-enemy.y)>radius)continue;
    target.lastChainExplosionId=chainId;
    target.health-=damage;
    showDamageNumber(target,damage);
    if(target.health<=0){
      target.health=0;
      target.chainReactionId=chainId;
      target.chainDepth=depth+1;
      target.chainNoChain=!!(suppressChains||boss);
      if(asteroids.includes(target))destroyAsteroid(target);
      else destroyEnemy(target);
    }
  }
}
function drawChainExplosionEffects(){
  const now=performance.now();
  for(const e of chainExplosionEffects){
    const p=1-e.life/e.maxLife,fade=Math.max(0,1-p),radius=e.maxRadius*(1-Math.pow(1-p,3));
    ctx.save();
    if(p<.24){const flash=ctx.createRadialGradient(e.x,e.y,0,e.x,e.y,e.maxRadius*.42);flash.addColorStop(0,"rgba(255,255,255,.95)");flash.addColorStop(.25,"rgba(194,250,255,.8)");flash.addColorStop(1,"rgba(84,233,255,0)");ctx.globalAlpha=Math.max(0,1-p/.24);ctx.fillStyle=flash;ctx.beginPath();ctx.arc(e.x,e.y,e.maxRadius*.42,0,Math.PI*2);ctx.fill()}
    ctx.globalAlpha=fade*.62;
    const orb=ctx.createRadialGradient(e.x,e.y,1,e.x,e.y,Math.max(2,radius));orb.addColorStop(0,"rgba(255,255,255,.95)");orb.addColorStop(.2,"rgba(190,250,255,.9)");orb.addColorStop(.55,"rgba(84,233,255,.7)");orb.addColorStop(1,"rgba(35,170,255,0)");ctx.fillStyle=orb;ctx.beginPath();ctx.arc(e.x,e.y,radius,0,Math.PI*2);ctx.fill();
    ctx.globalAlpha=fade;ctx.strokeStyle="#54e9ff";ctx.shadowColor="#54e9ff";ctx.shadowBlur=e.boss?30:18;ctx.lineWidth=(e.boss?7:4)*(1-p)+1;ctx.beginPath();ctx.arc(e.x,e.y,radius,0,Math.PI*2);ctx.stroke();
    if(e.depth>=3){ctx.globalAlpha=fade*.75;ctx.lineWidth=2+Math.min(5,e.depth);ctx.beginPath();ctx.arc(e.x,e.y,radius*.78,0,Math.PI*2);ctx.stroke()}
    ctx.restore();
  }
}
function destroyEnemy(enemy){
  if(!enemy||enemy.health>0)return;
  spawnDeadlyLootDrops({kind:"enemy",boss:!!enemy.boss,x:enemy.x,y:enemy.y});
  enemy.health=0;
  if(chainExplosionLevel>0){
    const chainId=enemy.chainReactionId||(++chainExplosionSequence);
    if(enemy.boss)triggerChainExplosion(enemy,chainId,true,true);
    else if(enemy.chainNoChain)triggerChainExplosion(enemy,chainId,false,true);
    else triggerChainExplosion(enemy,chainId,true,false);
  }
  const ach=getAchievementStats();
  if(enemy.boss){ach.bosses++;if(difficulty==="impossible")runImpossibleBossKills++;if(enemy.bigBoss)ach.splitBosses++;if(enemy.specialBoss)ach.specialBosses++;if(difficulty==="impossible")ach.impossibleBosses++;}else{ach.enemies++;runEnemiesKilled++;if(enemy.ultraLaserHit)runUltraLaserKills++;}
  saveAchievementStats(ach);
  checkAchievements();
  explosion(enemy.x,enemy.y);
  spawnEnemyDeathFragments(enemy);
  unlockCollectionItem(collectionIdForEntity(enemy,false));
  if(enemy.splitOnDeath&&!enemy.clone)splitEntityIntoClones(enemy);
  if(enemy.specialType==="mirror"&&!enemy.mirrorClone){const savedHealth=enemy.health;enemy.health=0;updateMirrorCloneThresholds(enemy);enemy.health=savedHealth;}
  if(enemy.boss){
    addBossSpeedCombo();
    if(enemy.specialType==="split"&&enemy.splitBoss){
      if(enemy.splitStage===0){
        addPlayerXP(100,true,"בוס מתפצל");
        for(let i=0;i<2;i++)enemies.push({boss:true,bigBoss:false,splitBoss:true,specialBoss:true,specialType:"split",x:enemy.x+(i===0?-80:80),y:enemy.y,health:enemy.maxHealth/2,maxHealth:enemy.maxHealth/2,direction:i===0?1:-1,timer:999999,splitStage:1,specialFireTimer:300,cloneSpawned:true});
        bossFightActive=true;
      }else if(enemy.splitStage===1){
        for(let i=0;i<2;i++)enemies.push({boss:true,bigBoss:false,splitBoss:true,specialBoss:true,specialType:"split",x:enemy.x+(i===0?-55:55),y:enemy.y,health:enemy.maxHealth/2,maxHealth:enemy.maxHealth/2,direction:i===0?1:-1,timer:999999,splitStage:2,miniSplitBoss:true,specialFireTimer:150,cloneSpawned:true});
        bossFightActive=true;
      }
    }else{
      addPlayerXP(50,true,"בוס");
      addXP(500+Math.floor(distance*.3));
      distanceSpeed*=1.35;
      bossSpeed*=1.15;
      showMessage("👹 הבוס הובס! ⚡ המשחק מהיר יותר!");
    }
  }else{
    addPlayerXP(7,true,"חללית אויב");
    addXP(40+Math.floor(distance*.02));
  }
  const stillBoss=enemies.some(e=>e.boss&&e.health>0);
  bossFightActive=stillBoss;
  if(!stillBoss){
    activeSpecialBossArea="";
    setSpecialBossArea("",false);
  }else{
    const remaining=enemies.find(e=>e.boss&&e.health>0&&e.specialBoss);
    if(remaining)setSpecialBossArea(remaining.specialType||"split",true);
  }
}

const INFERNO_BOMB_BASE={radius:100,directDamage:200,pulseDamage:20,durationMs:4000,exitPulses:5};
function infernoBombScale(){return 1+.25*Math.max(0,infernoBombLevel-1)}
function infernoBombCooldownMs(){return Math.max(1000,5000-500*Math.max(0,infernoBombLevel-1))}
function infernoBombAmmoChance(){return Math.min(64,10+3*infernoBombLevel+2*deadlyLootLevel+luckLevel)}
function infernoTargetRadius(target,isAsteroid){return isAsteroid?(Number(target.size)||16):(target.boss?55:(Number(target.size)||25))}
function infernoApplyDamage(target,amount){if(!target||target.health<=0||!(amount>0))return 0;const actual=Math.min(target.health,amount);target.health-=actual;showDamageNumber(target,actual);return actual}
function triggerBombEffect(){if(!bombEffectEnabled)return;const overlay=document.getElementById("bombEffectOverlay");if(!overlay)return;overlay.classList.remove("active");void overlay.offsetWidth;overlay.classList.add("active")}
function infernoCreateFireZone(x,y,now){
 const scale=infernoBombScale(),radius=INFERNO_BOMB_BASE.radius*scale;
 const zone={x,y,radius,damage:INFERNO_BOMB_BASE.pulseDamage*scale,expiresAt:now+INFERNO_BOMB_BASE.durationMs*scale,nextTickAt:now+500,targets:new Map()};
 for(const target of [...enemies,...asteroids]){if(target.health>0&&Math.hypot(target.x-x,target.y-y)<=radius+infernoTargetRadius(target,asteroids.includes(target)))infernoApplyDamage(target,INFERNO_BOMB_BASE.directDamage*scale)}
 if(bombEffectEnabled)infernoExplosionVisuals.push({x,y,radius,startAt:now,duration:1000});triggerBombEffect();
 infernoFireZones.push(zone);
}
function infernoBeginExitBurn(target,totalDamage,now){if(totalDamage>0&&target&&target.health>0)infernoExitBurns.push({target,damagePerPulse:totalDamage*.5/INFERNO_BOMB_BASE.exitPulses,pulsesLeft:INFERNO_BOMB_BASE.exitPulses,nextAt:now+1000})}
function updateInfernoBombs(){
 if(infernoBombLevel<=0)return;
 const now=performance.now();
 if(infernoBombPending&&now>=infernoBombPending.launchAt){
  infernoBombProjectiles.push({x:player.x,y:player.y,targetX:infernoBombPending.x,targetY:infernoBombPending.y,speed:14});
  infernoBombPending=null;
 }
 for(let i=infernoBombProjectiles.length-1;i>=0;i--){
  const p=infernoBombProjectiles[i],dx=p.targetX-p.x,dy=p.targetY-p.y,distance=Math.hypot(dx,dy);
  if(distance<=p.speed){infernoCreateFireZone(p.targetX,p.targetY,now);infernoBombProjectiles.splice(i,1)}
  else{p.x+=dx/distance*p.speed;p.y+=dy/distance*p.speed}
 }
 for(let i=infernoFireZones.length-1;i>=0;i--){
  const zone=infernoFireZones[i],inside=new Set();
  for(const target of [...enemies,...asteroids]){
   if(target.health<=0)continue;
   if(Math.hypot(target.x-zone.x,target.y-zone.y)<=zone.radius+infernoTargetRadius(target,asteroids.includes(target))){inside.add(target);if(!zone.targets.has(target))zone.targets.set(target,0)}
  }
  for(const [target,total] of zone.targets){if(!inside.has(target)){infernoBeginExitBurn(target,total,now);zone.targets.delete(target)}}
  if(now>=zone.nextTickAt){
   for(const target of inside){const actual=infernoApplyDamage(target,zone.damage);zone.targets.set(target,(zone.targets.get(target)||0)+actual)}
   zone.nextTickAt+=500;
  }
  if(now>=zone.expiresAt){for(const [target,total] of zone.targets)infernoBeginExitBurn(target,total,now);infernoFireZones.splice(i,1)}
 }
 for(let i=infernoExitBurns.length-1;i>=0;i--){
  const burn=infernoExitBurns[i];if(!burn.target||burn.target.health<=0){infernoExitBurns.splice(i,1);continue}
  if(now>=burn.nextAt){infernoApplyDamage(burn.target,burn.damagePerPulse);burn.pulsesLeft--;burn.nextAt+=1000;if(burn.pulsesLeft<=0)infernoExitBurns.splice(i,1)}
 }
 for(let i=infernoExplosionVisuals.length-1;i>=0;i--)if(now-infernoExplosionVisuals[i].startAt>=infernoExplosionVisuals[i].duration)infernoExplosionVisuals.splice(i,1);
}
function tryThrowInfernoBomb(event){
 if(currentWeapon!=="infernoBomb"||infernoBombLevel<=0||!gameRunning||paused||levelUpOpen||gameOverScreen.style.display==="flex")return;
 const now=performance.now();if(infernoBombPending||now-infernoBombLastThrowAt<infernoBombCooldownMs())return;
 if(infernoBombAmmo<=0){showMessage("💣 אין פצצות זמינות!");return}
 const rect=canvas.getBoundingClientRect(),x=(event.clientX-rect.left)*(canvas.width/rect.width),y=(event.clientY-rect.top)*(canvas.height/rect.height);
 infernoBombAmmo--;infernoBombLastThrowAt=now;infernoBombPending={x,y,launchAt:now+500};updateHUD();
}
function drawInfernoBombEffects(){
 const now=performance.now();
 for(const zone of infernoFireZones){const pulse=.88+.12*Math.sin(now/90);ctx.save();ctx.globalAlpha=.24;ctx.fillStyle="#ff3b0b";ctx.shadowColor="#ff4b00";ctx.shadowBlur=34;ctx.beginPath();ctx.arc(zone.x,zone.y,zone.radius*pulse,0,Math.PI*2);ctx.fill();ctx.globalAlpha=.85;ctx.strokeStyle="#ff9b22";ctx.lineWidth=3;ctx.stroke();ctx.restore()}
 for(const p of infernoBombProjectiles){ctx.save();ctx.fillStyle="#1a0a0a";ctx.strokeStyle="#ff3838";ctx.lineWidth=3;ctx.shadowColor="#ff1717";ctx.shadowBlur=18;ctx.beginPath();ctx.arc(p.x,p.y,10,0,Math.PI*2);ctx.fill();ctx.stroke();ctx.restore()}
 for(const e of infernoExplosionVisuals){const progress=(now-e.startAt)/e.duration;ctx.save();ctx.globalAlpha=1-progress;ctx.strokeStyle="#ffd54f";ctx.lineWidth=8*(1-progress)+1;ctx.shadowColor="#ff3d00";ctx.shadowBlur=28;ctx.beginPath();ctx.arc(e.x,e.y,e.radius*(.55+.45*progress),0,Math.PI*2);ctx.stroke();ctx.restore()}
 if(infernoBombLevel>0&&infernoBombAmmo>0&&now-infernoBombLastThrowAt>=infernoBombCooldownMs()&&!infernoBombPending){
  const scale=infernoBombScale(),radius=INFERNO_BOMB_BASE.radius*scale;ctx.save();ctx.globalAlpha=.9;ctx.strokeStyle="#ff3030";ctx.fillStyle="rgba(255,25,25,.12)";ctx.lineWidth=2;ctx.setLineDash([8,6]);ctx.beginPath();ctx.arc(mouse.x,mouse.y,radius,0,Math.PI*2);ctx.fill();ctx.stroke();ctx.setLineDash([]);ctx.beginPath();ctx.moveTo(mouse.x-12,mouse.y);ctx.lineTo(mouse.x+12,mouse.y);ctx.moveTo(mouse.x,mouse.y-12);ctx.lineTo(mouse.x,mouse.y+12);ctx.stroke();ctx.restore()
 }
 if(infernoBombPending){ctx.save();ctx.strokeStyle="#ff3030";ctx.lineWidth=3;ctx.beginPath();ctx.arc(infernoBombPending.x,infernoBombPending.y,INFERNO_BOMB_BASE.radius*infernoBombScale(),0,Math.PI*2);ctx.stroke();ctx.restore()}
}

function update(){if(!gameRunning)return;gameTime+=1/60;updateDeadlyLootPickups();updateInfernoBombs();spendUltraModePoints();for(let i=meteoriteFragments.length-1;i>=0;i--){const f=meteoriteFragments[i];f.x+=f.vx;f.y+=f.vy;f.vx*=.985;f.vy=f.vy*.985+.035;f.rotation+=f.spin;f.life--;if(f.life<=0)meteoriteFragments.splice(i,1)}let speed=effectiveMoveSpeed();
  const inv=controlsInverted;
  const left=keys.arrowleft||keys.a,right=keys.arrowright||keys.d,up=keys.arrowup||keys.w,down=keys.arrowdown||keys.s;
  if(!inv){if(left)player.x-=speed;if(right)player.x+=speed;if(up)player.y-=speed;if(down)player.y+=speed;}
  else{if(left)player.x+=speed;if(right)player.x-=speed;if(up)player.y+=speed;if(down)player.y-=speed;}
  player.x=Math.max(30,Math.min(canvas.width-30,player.x));player.y=Math.max(100,Math.min(canvas.height-50,player.y));
  if(keys.space)shoot();updateLaser();updateUltraLaser();updateFlamethrower();updateFlamethrowerBurn();updateSwords();updateDrones();updateBlackHoles();updatePoison();updateBurn();
  if(!bossFightActive){
    const oldDistance=Math.floor(distance);
    const worldMultiplier=worldSpeedMultiplier();
    const distanceDelta=distanceSpeed*worldMultiplier*(ultraModeActive?2:1);
    distance+=distanceDelta;
    updateSpeedComboDistance(distanceDelta);
    const newDistance=Math.floor(distance);
    if(newDistance>oldDistance){
      const oldFive=Math.floor(oldDistance/5),newFive=Math.floor(newDistance/5);
      if(newFive>oldFive)addPlayerXP(newFive-oldFive,true,"מרחק");
      if(personalBestAtStart>0&&newDistance>personalBestAtStart){
        const from=Math.max(oldDistance+1,personalBestAtStart+1);
        const recordMeters=Math.max(0,newDistance-from+1);
        if(recordMeters>0)addPlayerXP(recordMeters,true,"שיא חדש");
      }
    }
    checkLiveRecord();
    let d=Math.floor(distance);
    if(d!==lastAchievementDistance){lastAchievementDistance=d;checkAchievements();}
    planNextBoss(d);
    if(pendingBossPlan&&d>=pendingBossPlan.distance){
      const plan=pendingBossPlan;
      pendingBossPlan=null;
      lastBossDistance=plan.distance;
      if(plan.special)spawnBoss("special:"+plan.type);else spawnBoss("normal");
    }
  }
  const spawnChance=Math.min(.15,.02+distance/25000);
  if(Math.random()<spawnChance)spawnAsteroid();
  if(Math.random()<spawnChance/3)spawnEnemy();
  if(invincible){invincibleTimer--;if(invincibleTimer<=0){invincible=false;invincibleTimer=0}}
  if(dodgeGhostTimer>0)dodgeGhostTimer--;

  // Regular entities move here; special movement is handled once in processSpecialEntities().
  asteroids.forEach(a=>{
    if(a.health<=0)return;
    updateMirrorCloneThresholds(a);
    if(updateStunnedEnemy(a))return;
    const freezeFactor=getEnemyFreezeFactor(a);
    if(a.specialType==="normal")a.y+=a.speed*worldSpeedMultiplier()*freezeFactor;
  });
  enemies.forEach(enemy=>{
    if(enemy.health<=0)return;
    updateMirrorCloneThresholds(enemy);
    const freezeFactor=getEnemyFreezeFactor(enemy);
    if(enemy.boss){
      if(updateStunnedEnemy(enemy))return;
      // Split bosses are updated in this main entity loop so they cannot be skipped by
      // the generic special-boss early return.
      if(enemy.specialType==="split"){
        const stage=Number(enemy.splitStage)||0;
        const moveFactor=stage===0?1:stage===1?1.25:1.5;
        const edge=stage===0?100:stage===1?65:45;
        enemy.x+=(Number(enemy.direction)||1)*bossSpeed*moveFactor*freezeFactor;
        if(enemy.x<edge){enemy.x=edge;enemy.direction=1}
        else if(enemy.x>canvas.width-edge){enemy.x=canvas.width-edge;enemy.direction=-1}
        enemy.specialFireTimer=(Number.isFinite(enemy.specialFireTimer)?enemy.specialFireTimer:(stage===0?600:stage===1?300:150))-freezeFactor;
        if(enemy.specialFireTimer<=0){
          const dx=player.x-enemy.x,dy=player.y-enemy.y,len=Math.hypot(dx,dy)||1;
          const shotSpeed=stage===0?2.5:stage===1?5:10;
          const shotSize=stage===0?18:stage===1?10:6;
          enemyBullets.push({freezeSpeedMultiplier:freezeFactor,x:enemy.x,y:enemy.y+shotSize,vx:dx/len*shotSpeed,vy:dy/len*shotSpeed,size:shotSize,specialSplitBullet:true,splitStage:stage,damage:stage===0?2:1});
          enemy.specialFireTimer=stage===0?600:stage===1?300:150;
        }
        return;
      }
      if(enemy.specialBoss)return;
      enemy.x+=enemy.direction*bossSpeed*freezeFactor;if(enemy.x<100||enemy.x>canvas.width-100)enemy.direction*=-1;
      enemy.timer-=freezeFactor;
      if(enemy.timer<=0){
        const dx=player.x-enemy.x,dy=player.y-enemy.y,len=Math.hypot(dx,dy)||1;
        const shots=difficultySettings[difficulty].enemyTriple?3:1;
        for(let ss=0;ss<shots;ss++){
          const spread=(ss-(shots-1)/2)*.25;
          enemyBullets.push({x:enemy.x,y:enemy.y+20,vx:(dx/len+spread)*2,vy:4,freezeSpeedMultiplier:freezeFactor});
        }
        enemy.timer=25;
      }
      return;
    }
    if(enemy.specialType==="normal"){
      if(updateStunnedEnemy(enemy))return;
      if(updateConfusedEnemy(enemy))return;
      enemy.y+=enemy.speed*worldSpeedMultiplier()*freezeFactor;
      enemy.x+=Math.sin(Date.now()/500)*.7*worldSpeedMultiplier()*freezeFactor;
      enemy.timer-=freezeFactor;
      if(enemy.timer<=0){
        const dx=player.x-enemy.x,dy=player.y-enemy.y,len=Math.hypot(dx,dy)||1;
        const shots=difficultySettings[difficulty].enemyTriple?3:1;
        for(let ss=0;ss<shots;ss++){
          const spread=(ss-(shots-1)/2)*.25;
          enemyBullets.push({x:enemy.x,y:enemy.y+20,vx:(dx/len+spread),vy:3,freezeSpeedMultiplier:freezeFactor});
        }
        enemy.timer=100;
      }
    }
  });
updateTeleportAreaGates();
processSpecialEntities();
  bullets.forEach(b=>{
    if(b.dead)return;
    const bulletSpeed=Math.hypot(Number(b.vx)||0,Number(b.vy)||0);
    b.x+=Number(b.vx)||0;
    b.y+=Number(b.vy)||0;
    if(b.splitChild){b.splitTravel=(Number(b.splitTravel)||0)+bulletSpeed;if(b.splitTravel>=(Number(b.splitMaxTravel)||900))b.dead=true;}
    if(b.dead)return;
    asteroids.forEach(a=>{
      if(a.health<=0||b.dead)return;
      if(Math.hypot(b.x-a.x,b.y-a.y)<a.size+b.size){
        if(b.blackHole){b.dead=true;spawnBlackHole(b.x,b.y);return}
        if(b.rpg){b.dead=true;rpgExplosion(b.x,b.y,b.rpgMini===true);return}
        if(shouldSplitBullet(b)){b.dead=true;spawnSplitBullets(b);return}
        const piercingHit=getPiercingHit(b,a);if(!piercingHit)return;
        const hitDamage=getContinuousFireDamage(a,b.damage*5*piercingHit.multiplier,b,performance.now());a.health-=hitDamage;showDamageNumber(a,hitDamage);applyPoison(a);applyFreeze(a);applyStun(a);if(!b.burnFailed&&a.health>0&&burnLevel>0&&!applyBurn(a,false,hitDamage))b.burnFailed=true;
        if(chainLightningLevel>0)triggerChainLightning(a);
        if(piercingHit.deadAfterHit)b.dead=true;
        if(a.health<=0)destroyAsteroid(a);
      }
    });
    if(b.dead)return;
    enemies.forEach(enemy=>{
      if(enemy.health<=0||b.dead)return;
      const size=enemy.boss?(enemy.specialType==="split"?(enemy.splitStage===2?38:62):55):25;
      if(Math.hypot(b.x-enemy.x,b.y-enemy.y)<size+b.size){
        if(b.blackHole){b.dead=true;spawnBlackHole(b.x,b.y);return}
        if(b.rpg){b.dead=true;rpgExplosion(b.x,b.y,b.rpgMini===true);return}
        if(shouldSplitBullet(b)){b.dead=true;spawnSplitBullets(b);return}
        const piercingHit=getPiercingHit(b,enemy);if(!piercingHit)return;
        const hitDamage=getContinuousFireDamage(enemy,b.damage*5*piercingHit.multiplier,b,performance.now());enemy.health-=hitDamage;showDamageNumber(enemy,hitDamage);applyPoison(enemy);applyFreeze(enemy);applyStun(enemy);if(!b.burnFailed&&enemy.health>0&&burnLevel>0&&!applyBurn(enemy,false,hitDamage))b.burnFailed=true;
        if(enemy.boss)screenShake(2,80);
        if(chainLightningLevel>0)triggerChainLightning(enemy);
        if(piercingHit.deadAfterHit)b.dead=true;
        if(enemy.health<=0)destroyEnemy(enemy);
      }
    });
  });
  enemyBullets.forEach(b=>{
    if(b.dead)return;
    const bulletSpeedMultiplier=Math.max(.1,Math.min(1,Number(b.freezeSpeedMultiplier)||1));
    b.x+=(Number(b.vx)||0)*bulletSpeedMultiplier;
    b.y+=(Number(b.vy)||0)*bulletSpeedMultiplier;
    if(b.shortRange){
      b.rangeLeft=(b.rangeLeft??240)-Math.hypot(Number(b.vx)||0,Number(b.vy)||0)*bulletSpeedMultiplier;
      if(b.rangeLeft<=0){b.dead=true;return}
    }
    if(b.confusedBullet){
      for(const target of enemies){
        if(target===b.confusedSource||target.health<=0)continue;
        if(Math.hypot(b.x-target.x,b.y-target.y)<(target.boss?55:25)+6){
          const confusionDamage=target.maxHealth*(Number(b.confusedDamage)||getConfusionDamageMultiplier());target.health-=confusionDamage;updateMirrorCloneThresholds(target,Math.max(0,target.health+confusionDamage),Math.max(0,target.health));
          b.dead=true;
          if(target.health<=0)destroyEnemy(target);
          break;
        }
      }
      return;
    }
    if(Math.hypot(b.x-player.x,b.y-player.y)<25){if(b.specialSplitBullet&&b.splitStage===0){
    const wasInv=invincible||ultraModeActive||dodgeGhostTimer>0;if(!wasInv){
      player.lives=Math.max(0,player.lives-2);
      resetTemporarySpeedCombo();
      b.dead=true;if(player.lives<=0){if(reviveAvailable){reviveAvailable=false;player.lives=player.maxLives;invincible=true;invincibleTimer=420;}else gameOver();}else{invincible=true;invincibleTimer=300;showMessage("💥 פגיעה חזקה! ❤️ "+player.lives);updateHUD();}}
  }else if(damagePlayer())b.dead=true}});asteroids.forEach(a=>{
  if(a.health<=0)return;
  if(a.teleportMarker||a.y<-500)return;
  if(Math.hypot(a.x-player.x,a.y-player.y)<a.size+20){
    if(invincible||ultraModeActive||dodgeGhostTimer>0)return;
    if(a.specialType==="ice"){freezeTimer=Math.max(freezeTimer,420);a.health=0;destroyAsteroid(a);return}
    if(a.chaserMeteor){damagePlayer();return}
    if(a.specialType==="inversion"){inversionTimer=Math.max(inversionTimer,120)}
    if(a.specialType==="chaser"){}
    a.health=0;damagePlayer();
  }
});
  enemies.forEach(enemy=>{
    if(enemy.health<=0||enemy.teleportMarker||enemy.y<-500)return;
    const size=enemy.boss?(enemy.specialType==="split"?(enemy.splitStage===2?38:62):55):25;
    if(Math.hypot(enemy.x-player.x,enemy.y-player.y)<size+20){
      if(invincible||ultraModeActive||dodgeGhostTimer>0)return;
      if(enemy.specialType==="ice"){if(damagePlayer())freezeTimer=Math.max(freezeTimer,120);return}
      if(enemy.specialType==="inversion"){damagePlayer();return}
      if(enemy.specialType==="chaser"){
        const hit=damagePlayer();
        if(hit&&!enemy.boss){enemy.chaserStraight=true;const len=Math.hypot(player.x-enemy.x,player.y-enemy.y)||1;enemy.chaserVx=(player.x-enemy.x)/len;enemy.chaserVy=(player.y-enemy.y)/len;}
        else if(hit&&enemy.boss)enemy.chaserReturning=true;
        return;
      }
      damagePlayer();
    }
  });bullets=bullets.filter(b=>!b.dead&&b.y>-100&&b.y<canvas.height+100&&b.x>-100&&b.x<canvas.width+100);enemyBullets=enemyBullets.filter(b=>!b.dead&&b.y>-100&&b.y<canvas.height+100&&b.x>-100&&b.x<canvas.width+100);asteroids=asteroids.filter(a=>a.health>0&&a.y<canvas.height+100);enemies=enemies.filter(e=>e.health>0&&(e.boss||e.y<canvas.height+100));explosions.forEach(e=>{e.radius+=3;e.life--});explosions=explosions.filter(e=>e.life>0);chainExplosionEffects.forEach(e=>e.life--);chainExplosionEffects=chainExplosionEffects.filter(e=>e.life>0);const boss=enemies.find(e=>e.boss);if(boss){document.getElementById("bossContainer").style.display="block";document.getElementById("bossHealth").style.width=Math.max(0,boss.health/boss.maxHealth*100)+"%"}else document.getElementById("bossContainer").style.display="none";updateHUD()}
function processSpecialEntities(){
  if(freezeTimer>0)freezeTimer--;
  if(inversionTimer>0)inversionTimer--;

  // Special bosses and their unique attacks.
  enemies.forEach(enemy=>{
    if(!enemy.boss||!enemy.specialBoss||enemy.health<=0||enemy.specialType==="split")return;
    const freezeFactor=getEnemyFreezeFactor(enemy);
    if(updateStunnedEnemy(enemy))return;
    if(enemy.specialType==="split"){
      const stage=enemy.splitStage||0;
      const splitMoveFactor=stage===0?1:stage===1?1.25:1.5;
      enemy.x+=enemy.direction*bossSpeed*splitMoveFactor*freezeFactor;
      const edge=stage===0?100:stage===1?65:45;
      if(enemy.x<edge){enemy.x=edge;enemy.direction=1}
      else if(enemy.x>canvas.width-edge){enemy.x=canvas.width-edge;enemy.direction=-1}
      enemy.specialFireTimer=(enemy.specialFireTimer??(stage===0?600:stage===1?300:150))-freezeFactor;
      if(enemy.specialFireTimer<=0){
        const dx=player.x-enemy.x,dy=player.y-enemy.y,len=Math.hypot(dx,dy)||1;
        const stage=enemy.splitStage||0;
        const speed=stage===0?2.5:stage===1?5:10;
        const size=stage===0?18:stage===1?10:6;
        enemyBullets.push({freezeSpeedMultiplier:freezeFactor,x:enemy.x,y:enemy.y+size,vx:dx/len*speed,vy:dy/len*speed,size,
          specialSplitBullet:true,splitStage:stage,damage:stage===0?2:1});
        enemy.specialFireTimer=stage===0?600:stage===1?300:150;
      }
      return;
    }
    if(enemy.specialType==="mirror"){
      updateMirrorCloneThresholds(enemy);enemy.x+=enemy.direction*bossSpeed*(enemy.mirrorClone?.5:1)*freezeFactor;
      if(enemy.x<100||enemy.x>canvas.width-100)enemy.direction*=-1;enemy.timer=(enemy.timer??30)-freezeFactor;
      if(enemy.timer<=0){const dx=player.x-enemy.x,dy=player.y-enemy.y,len=Math.hypot(dx,dy)||1,shotSpeed=enemy.mirrorClone?1:2;enemyBullets.push({freezeSpeedMultiplier:freezeFactor,speedMultiplier:enemy.mirrorClone?.5:1,cloneBullet:!!enemy.mirrorClone,x:enemy.x,y:enemy.y+20,vx:dx/len*shotSpeed,vy:enemy.mirrorClone?1.5:3});enemy.timer=enemy.mirrorClone?200:30;}return;
    }
    if(enemy.specialType==="ice"){
      enemy.x+=enemy.direction*bossSpeed*.9*freezeFactor;
      if(enemy.x<100||enemy.x>canvas.width-100)enemy.direction*=-1;
      enemy.timer=(enemy.timer??45)-freezeFactor;
      if(enemy.timer<=0){
        const dx=player.x-enemy.x,dy=player.y-enemy.y,len=Math.hypot(dx,dy)||1;
        enemyBullets.push({freezeSpeedMultiplier:freezeFactor,x:enemy.x,y:enemy.y+20,vx:dx/len*2,vy:3});
        enemy.timer=45;
      }
      return;
    }
    if(enemy.specialType==="gates"){
      enemy.gateTimer=(enemy.gateTimer??120)-freezeFactor;
      if(enemy.gateTimer<=0){
        enemy.gateTimer=180;
        enemy.x=80+Math.random()*(canvas.width-160);
        enemy.y=140+Math.random()*Math.max(80,canvas.height-300);
      }
      enemy.timer=(enemy.timer??60)-freezeFactor;
      if(enemy.timer<=0){
        const dx=player.x-enemy.x,dy=player.y-enemy.y,len=Math.hypot(dx,dy)||1;
        enemyBullets.push({freezeSpeedMultiplier:freezeFactor,x:enemy.x,y:enemy.y+20,vx:dx/len*2,vy:3});
        enemy.timer=60;
      }
      return;
    }
    if(enemy.specialType==="chaser"){
      if(enemy.chaserReturning){const rdx=enemy.startX-enemy.x,rdy=enemy.startY-enemy.y,rlen=Math.hypot(rdx,rdy)||1,returnSpeed=Math.max(2.5,bossSpeed*1.35)*worldSpeedMultiplier()*freezeFactor;enemy.x+=rdx/rlen*returnSpeed;enemy.y+=rdy/rlen*returnSpeed;if(rlen<=returnSpeed){enemy.x=enemy.startX;enemy.y=enemy.startY;}if(!invincible&&!ultraModeActive&&dodgeGhostTimer<=0)enemy.chaserReturning=false;return;}
      const dx=player.x-enemy.x,dy=player.y-enemy.y,len=Math.hypot(dx,dy)||1,chaseSpeed=Math.max(3.5,bossSpeed*1.45);enemy.x+=dx/len*chaseSpeed*worldSpeedMultiplier()*freezeFactor;enemy.y+=dy/len*chaseSpeed*worldSpeedMultiplier()*freezeFactor;
      const fireRange=330;enemy.timer=(enemy.timer??45)-freezeFactor;if(Math.hypot(dx,dy)<=fireRange&&enemy.timer<=0){for(let ss=0;ss<3;ss++){const base=Math.atan2(dy,dx)+(ss-1)*.16;enemyBullets.push({freezeSpeedMultiplier:freezeFactor,x:enemy.x,y:enemy.y+20,vx:Math.cos(base)*4.5,vy:Math.sin(base)*4.5,shortRange:true,rangeLeft:fireRange});}enemy.timer=50;}return;
    }
    if(enemy.specialType==="teleport"){
      enemy.specialTimer=(enemy.specialTimer??240)-freezeFactor;
      if(!enemy.teleportHidden&&enemy.specialTimer<=0){
        enemy.specialTimer=240;
        enemy.teleportMarker={x:70+Math.random()*(canvas.width-140),y:140+Math.random()*Math.max(60,canvas.height-300),life:240};
        enemy.teleportHidden=true;
      }
      if(enemy.teleportHidden&&enemy.teleportMarker){
        enemy.teleportMarker.life-=freezeFactor;
        if(enemy.teleportMarker.life<=0){
          enemy.x=enemy.teleportMarker.x;enemy.y=enemy.teleportMarker.y;
          enemy.teleportMarker=null;enemy.teleportHidden=false;enemy.timer=5;
        }
      }
      if(!enemy.teleportHidden){
        enemy.timer=(enemy.timer??5)-freezeFactor;
        if(enemy.timer<=0){
          const dx=player.x-enemy.x,dy=player.y-enemy.y,len=Math.hypot(dx,dy)||1;
          enemyBullets.push({freezeSpeedMultiplier:freezeFactor,x:enemy.x,y:enemy.y+20,vx:dx/len*2.2,vy:3.5});
          enemy.timer=120;
        }
      }
      return;
    }
    if(enemy.specialType==="inversion"){
      if(enemy.inversionChargeTimer>0){enemy.inversionChargeTimer=Math.max(0,enemy.inversionChargeTimer-freezeFactor);enemy.x+=enemy.direction*bossSpeed*.095*freezeFactor;if(enemy.inversionChargeTimer<=0){controlsInverted=!controlsInverted;enemy.inversionCooldownTimer=360+Math.random()*240;enemy.timer=50;showMessage(controlsInverted?"🔄 המקשים התהפכו!":"🔄 המקשים חזרו למצב רגיל!");}return;}
      enemy.inversionCooldownTimer=(enemy.inversionCooldownTimer??(360+Math.random()*240))-freezeFactor;if(enemy.inversionCooldownTimer<=0){enemy.inversionChargeTimer=120;enemy.timer=50;return;}
      enemy.x+=enemy.direction*bossSpeed*.95*freezeFactor;
      if(enemy.x<100||enemy.x>canvas.width-100)enemy.direction*=-1;
      enemy.timer=(enemy.timer??50)-freezeFactor;
      if(enemy.timer<=0){
        const dx=player.x-enemy.x,dy=player.y-enemy.y,len=Math.hypot(dx,dy)||1;
        enemyBullets.push({freezeSpeedMultiplier:freezeFactor,x:enemy.x,y:enemy.y+20,vx:dx/len*2,vy:3});
        enemy.timer=50;
      }
    }
    // king/doom special bosses keep their own area but use the ordinary boss attack cadence.
    if(enemy.specialType==="king"||enemy.specialType==="doom"){
      enemy.x+=enemy.direction*bossSpeed*freezeFactor;if(enemy.x<100||enemy.x>canvas.width-100)enemy.direction*=-1;
      const healthRatio=Math.max(0,Math.min(1,enemy.health/Math.max(1,enemy.maxHealth))),fireCooldown=enemy.specialType==="doom"?Math.max(8,70*(.2+.8*healthRatio)):35,shotSpeed=enemy.specialType==="doom"?2.2+(1-healthRatio)*3:2.2;
      enemy.timer=(enemy.timer??fireCooldown)-freezeFactor;if(enemy.timer<=0){const dx=player.x-enemy.x,dy=player.y-enemy.y,len=Math.hypot(dx,dy)||1;enemyBullets.push({freezeSpeedMultiplier:freezeFactor,x:enemy.x,y:enemy.y+20,vx:dx/len*shotSpeed,vy:4+(1-healthRatio)*1.5});enemy.timer=fireCooldown;}
    }
  });

  // Special enemies.
  enemies.forEach(enemy=>{
    if(enemy.boss||enemy.health<=0)return;
    const freezeFactor=getEnemyFreezeFactor(enemy);
    if(updateStunnedEnemy(enemy))return;
    if(updateConfusedEnemy(enemy))return;
    if(enemy.specialType==="mirror"){
      enemy.y+=enemy.speed*(enemy.mirrorClone?.5:1)*worldSpeedMultiplier()*freezeFactor;
      enemy.x+=Math.sin(Date.now()/500)*.7*(enemy.mirrorClone?.5:1)*freezeFactor;
      enemy.timer=(enemy.timer??100)-(enemy.mirrorClone?2:1)*freezeFactor;
      if(enemy.timer<=0){const dx=player.x-enemy.x,dy=player.y-enemy.y,len=Math.hypot(dx,dy)||1;enemyBullets.push({freezeSpeedMultiplier:freezeFactor,speedMultiplier:enemy.mirrorClone?.5:1,cloneBullet:!!enemy.mirrorClone,x:enemy.x,y:enemy.y+20,vx:dx/len*(enemy.mirrorClone?.5:1),vy:enemy.mirrorClone?1.5:3});enemy.timer=enemy.mirrorClone?200:100;}
    }else if(enemy.specialType==="chaser"){
      if(enemy.chaserStraight){enemy.x+=(Number(enemy.chaserVx)||0)*freezeFactor;enemy.y+=(Number(enemy.chaserVy)||0)*freezeFactor;}
      else{const dx=player.x-enemy.x,dy=player.y-enemy.y,len=Math.hypot(dx,dy)||1,chaseSpeed=Math.max(3.2,enemy.speed*1.05);enemy.x+=dx/len*chaseSpeed*freezeFactor;enemy.y+=dy/len*chaseSpeed*freezeFactor;const fireRange=240;enemy.timer=(enemy.timer??70)-freezeFactor;if(Math.hypot(dx,dy)<=fireRange&&enemy.timer<=0){for(let ss=0;ss<2;ss++){const base=Math.atan2(dy,dx)+(ss-.5)*.16;enemyBullets.push({freezeSpeedMultiplier:freezeFactor,x:enemy.x,y:enemy.y+20,vx:Math.cos(base)*4,vy:Math.sin(base)*4,shortRange:true,rangeLeft:fireRange});}enemy.timer=70;}}
    }else if(enemy.specialType==="ice"){
      enemy.y+=enemy.speed*worldSpeedMultiplier()*freezeFactor;enemy.x+=Math.sin(Date.now()/500)*.7*worldSpeedMultiplier()*freezeFactor;
      enemy.timer-=freezeFactor;
      if(enemy.timer<=0){
        const dx=player.x-enemy.x,dy=player.y-enemy.y,len=Math.hypot(dx,dy)||1;
        enemyBullets.push({freezeSpeedMultiplier:freezeFactor,x:enemy.x,y:enemy.y+20,vx:dx/len,vy:3});
        enemy.timer=100;
      }
    }else if(enemy.specialType==="teleport"){
      if(!enemy.teleportHidden){
        enemy.teleportTimer=(enemy.teleportTimer??240)-freezeFactor;
        if(enemy.teleportTimer<=0){
          enemy.teleportTimer=240;
          enemy.teleportMarker={x:50+Math.random()*(canvas.width-100),y:120+Math.random()*(canvas.height-220),life:240};
          enemy.teleportHidden=true;
        }
        if(enemy.timer>0)enemy.timer-=freezeFactor;
        if(enemy.timer<=0){
          const dx=player.x-enemy.x,dy=player.y-enemy.y,len=Math.hypot(dx,dy)||1;
          enemyBullets.push({freezeSpeedMultiplier:freezeFactor,x:enemy.x,y:enemy.y+20,vx:dx/len,vy:3});
          enemy.timer=100;
        }
      }else if(enemy.teleportMarker){
        enemy.teleportMarker.life-=freezeFactor;
        if(enemy.teleportMarker.life<=0){
          enemy.x=enemy.teleportMarker.x;enemy.y=enemy.teleportMarker.y;
          enemy.teleportMarker=null;enemy.teleportHidden=false;enemy.timer=5;
        }
      }
    }else if(enemy.specialType==="gate"){
      enemy.y+=enemy.speed*worldSpeedMultiplier();enemy.gateTimer=(enemy.gateTimer??180)-freezeFactor;
      if(enemy.gateTimer<=0){enemy.gateTimer=180;enemy.x=70+Math.random()*(canvas.width-140);enemy.y=120+Math.random()*Math.max(80,canvas.height-280);}
      enemy.timer-=freezeFactor;
      if(enemy.timer<=0){
        const dx=player.x-enemy.x,dy=player.y-enemy.y,len=Math.hypot(dx,dy)||1;
        enemyBullets.push({freezeSpeedMultiplier:freezeFactor,x:enemy.x,y:enemy.y+20,vx:dx/len,vy:3});enemy.timer=100;
      }
    }else if(enemy.specialType==="inversion"){
      enemy.y+=enemy.speed*worldSpeedMultiplier();enemy.timer-=freezeFactor;if(enemy.timer<=0){const dx=player.x-enemy.x,dy=player.y-enemy.y,len=Math.hypot(dx,dy)||1;enemyBullets.push({freezeSpeedMultiplier:freezeFactor,x:enemy.x,y:enemy.y+20,vx:dx/len,vy:3});enemy.timer=100;}
    }else if(enemy.specialType==="doom"){
      enemy.y+=enemy.speed*worldSpeedMultiplier()*freezeFactor;const healthRatio=Math.max(0,Math.min(1,enemy.health/Math.max(1,enemy.maxHealth))),fireCooldown=Math.max(12,70*(.2+.8*healthRatio)),shotSpeed=2.5+(1-healthRatio)*3;enemy.timer=(enemy.timer??fireCooldown)-freezeFactor;if(enemy.timer<=0){const dx=player.x-enemy.x,dy=player.y-enemy.y,len=Math.hypot(dx,dy)||1;enemyBullets.push({freezeSpeedMultiplier:freezeFactor,x:enemy.x,y:enemy.y+20,vx:dx/len*shotSpeed,vy:3.5+(1-healthRatio)*1.5});enemy.timer=fireCooldown;}
    }else if(enemy.specialType!=="normal"){
      enemy.y+=enemy.speed*freezeFactor;
    }
  });

  // Special meteor movement/effects.
  asteroids.forEach(a=>{
    if(a.health<=0)return;
    if(updateStunnedEnemy(a))return;
    const freezeFactor=getEnemyFreezeFactor(a);
    if(a.specialType==="mirror"){
      a.y+=a.speed*worldSpeedMultiplier()*freezeFactor;
    }else if(a.specialType==="ice"){
      a.y+=a.speed*freezeFactor;
    }else if(a.specialType==="gate"){
      a.y+=a.speed*worldSpeedMultiplier()*freezeFactor;
      a.gateTimer=(a.gateTimer??180)-freezeFactor;
      if(a.gateTimer<=0){a.gateTimer=180;a.x=70+Math.random()*(canvas.width-140);a.y=120+Math.random()*Math.max(80,canvas.height-280);}
    }else if(a.specialType==="chaser"||a.chaserMeteor){
      if(a.chaserMeteor){a.x+=(Number(a.vx)||0)*a.speed*worldSpeedMultiplier()*freezeFactor;a.y+=(Number(a.vy)||0)*a.speed*worldSpeedMultiplier()*freezeFactor;}
      else{const dx=player.x-a.x,dy=player.y-a.y,len=Math.hypot(dx,dy)||1;a.x+=dx/len*a.speed*worldSpeedMultiplier()*freezeFactor;a.y+=dy/len*a.speed*worldSpeedMultiplier()*freezeFactor;}
    }else if(a.specialType==="teleport"){
      if(!a.teleportHidden){
        a.teleportTimer=(a.teleportTimer??240)-freezeFactor;
        if(a.teleportTimer<=0){
          a.teleportTimer=240;
          a.teleportMarker={x:50+Math.random()*(canvas.width-100),y:120+Math.random()*(canvas.height-220),life:240};
          a.teleportHidden=true;
        }
      }else if(a.teleportMarker){
        a.teleportMarker.life-=freezeFactor;
        if(a.teleportMarker.life<=0){
          a.x=a.teleportMarker.x;a.y=a.teleportMarker.y;a.teleportMarker=null;a.teleportHidden=false;a.shatterTimer=15;
        }
      }
      if(a.shatterTimer>0){a.shatterTimer--;if(a.shatterTimer===0){explosion(a.x,a.y);if(Math.hypot(a.x-player.x,a.y-player.y)<110)damagePlayer();}}
    }else if(a.specialType==="inversion"){
      if(!a.inversionReturning){
        a.y+=a.speed*worldSpeedMultiplier()*freezeFactor;
        if(a.y>=canvas.height-a.size){a.y=canvas.height-a.size;a.inversionReturning=true;}
      }else{
        a.y-=a.speed*worldSpeedMultiplier()*freezeFactor;
        if(a.y<-a.size)a.health=0;
      }
    }
  });

  // Chaser-area warning zones: 5 seconds, then a very fast meteor flies through them.
  if(activeSpecialBossArea==="chaser"){
    window.chaserHazards=window.chaserHazards||[];
    if(window.chaserHazards.length<1&&Math.random()<.004)window.chaserHazards.push({x:40+Math.random()*(canvas.width-80),y:150,rx:58,ry:28,life:300,spawned:false});
    window.chaserHazards.forEach(h=>{h.life--;if(h.life<=0&&!h.spawned){h.spawned=true;const dx=player.x-h.x,dy=player.y-h.y,len=Math.hypot(dx,dy)||1;asteroids.push({x:h.x,y:-60,size:18,health:999999,maxHealth:999999,speed:18+distance/4000,vx:dx/len,vy:dy/len,specialType:"chaserMeteor",chaserMeteor:true,splitOnDeath:false,clone:false,teleportTimer:0,teleportMarker:null,teleportHidden:false,gateTimer:180,inversionReturning:false,shatterTimer:0});}});
    window.chaserHazards=window.chaserHazards.filter(h=>!h.spawned);
  }

  // Split-bullet wall logic.
  enemyBullets.forEach(b=>{
    if(!b.specialSplitBullet||b.dead)return;
    if(b.x<=b.size||b.x>=canvas.width-b.size||b.y<=b.size||b.y>=canvas.height-b.size){
      if(b.splitStage===0){
        const a=Math.atan2(b.vy,b.vx);for(const delta of [-.35,.35])enemyBullets.push({freezeSpeedMultiplier:freezeFactor,x:b.x,y:b.y,vx:Math.cos(a+delta)*5,vy:Math.sin(a+delta)*5,size:10,specialSplitBullet:true,splitStage:1,damage:1});
      }else if(b.splitStage===1){
        const a=Math.atan2(b.vy,b.vx);for(const delta of [-.28,.28])enemyBullets.push({freezeSpeedMultiplier:freezeFactor,x:b.x,y:b.y,vx:Math.cos(a+delta)*10,vy:Math.sin(a+delta)*10,size:6,specialSplitBullet:true,splitStage:2,damage:1});
      }
      b.dead=true;
    }
  });
}

function drawHealthBar(x,y,width,height,health,maxHealth){const ratio=Math.max(0,Math.min(1,health/maxHealth));ctx.fillStyle="rgba(0,0,0,.85)";ctx.fillRect(x-width/2,y,width,height);ctx.fillStyle=ratio>.5?"#00e676":ratio>.25?"#ffeb3b":"#ff1744";ctx.fillRect(x-width/2,y,width*ratio,height);ctx.strokeStyle="#fff";ctx.strokeRect(x-width/2,y,width,height)}

function draw(){ctx.clearRect(0,0,canvas.width,canvas.height);drawSpaceBackground();drawSpaceDust();drawInfernoBombEffects();ctx.save();ctx.translate(player.x,player.y);const shieldBubbleVisible=ultraShields>0||performance.now()<shieldBubbleFlashUntil;if(shieldBubbleVisible){const flashing=performance.now()<shieldBubbleFlashUntil,pulse=flashing?(Math.floor(performance.now()/130)%2===0):false;ctx.save();ctx.globalAlpha=flashing?(pulse?.78:.22):.34;ctx.fillStyle=pulse?"#e7fbff":"#36bfff";ctx.strokeStyle=pulse?"#ffffff":"#35baff";ctx.lineWidth=pulse?4:2.5;ctx.shadowColor="#18aaff";ctx.shadowBlur=pulse?34:22;ctx.beginPath();ctx.arc(0,0,41+(pulse?2:0),0,Math.PI*2);ctx.fill();ctx.stroke();ctx.restore()}const ghostActive=invincible||ultraModeActive||dodgeGhostTimer>0;ctx.globalAlpha=ghostActive?.68:1;if(dodgeGhostTimer>0&&!invincible){ctx.globalAlpha=.68}const rageActive=lowHealthActive()||deadlyLootRageActive();if(ultraModeActive){ctx.fillStyle="#ffffff";ctx.shadowColor="#c77dff";ctx.shadowBlur=45}else if(rageActive){ctx.fillStyle="#ff8c00";ctx.shadowColor="#ff8c00";ctx.shadowBlur=35}else{ctx.fillStyle="#4cc9f0";ctx.shadowBlur=0}ctx.beginPath();ctx.moveTo(0,-30);ctx.lineTo(-22,25);ctx.lineTo(0,15);ctx.lineTo(22,25);ctx.closePath();ctx.fill();ctx.fillStyle="#fff";ctx.fillRect(-5,-10,10,15);ctx.restore();if(deadlyLootRageActive()){ctx.save();ctx.font="bold 15px Arial";ctx.textAlign="center";ctx.fillStyle="#ffb347";ctx.shadowColor="#ff6d00";ctx.shadowBlur=12;ctx.fillText("🔥 "+Math.max(0,(deadlyLootRageUntil-performance.now())/1000).toFixed(1)+"s",player.x,player.y-48);ctx.restore()}if(laserMode&&laserOn){const dx=mouse.x-player.x,dy=mouse.y-player.y,len=Math.hypot(dx,dy)||1;ctx.save();const maxHot=laserHeatLevel>0&&laserHeat>=.999,laserColor=maxHot?(Math.floor(performance.now()/220)%2?"#ff1010":"#ff4a4a"):(laserHeatLevel>0?getLaserHeatColor():"#ff1744");ctx.strokeStyle=laserColor;ctx.shadowColor=laserColor;ctx.shadowBlur=20+laserHeat*25;ctx.lineWidth=12+laserHeat*3;ctx.beginPath();ctx.moveTo(player.x,player.y);ctx.lineTo(player.x+dx/len*1000,player.y+dy/len*1000);ctx.stroke();ctx.restore()}
drawUltraLaser();if(laserHeatLevel>0){const barW=72,barH=7,barX=player.x-barW/2,barY=player.y+39,maxHot=laserHeat>=.999,barColor=maxHot?(Math.floor(performance.now()/220)%2?"#ff1010":"#ff4a4a"):getLaserHeatColor();ctx.save();ctx.fillStyle="rgba(0,0,0,.8)";ctx.fillRect(barX-2,barY-2,barW+4,barH+4);ctx.fillStyle="#252b38";ctx.fillRect(barX,barY,barW,barH);ctx.fillStyle=barColor;ctx.shadowColor=barColor;ctx.shadowBlur=maxHot?14:5;ctx.fillRect(barX,barY,barW*laserHeat,barH);ctx.strokeStyle="#fff";ctx.lineWidth=1;ctx.strokeRect(barX,barY,barW,barH);ctx.restore()}if(flamethrowerOn){const dx=mouse.x-player.x,dy=mouse.y-player.y,len=Math.hypot(dx,dy)||1,dirX=dx/len,dirY=dy/len;ctx.save();ctx.globalAlpha=.8;ctx.shadowColor="#ff9800";ctx.shadowBlur=25;ctx.strokeStyle="#ff6d00";ctx.lineWidth=55;ctx.beginPath();ctx.moveTo(player.x+dirX*20,player.y+dirY*20);ctx.lineTo(player.x+dirX*220,player.y+dirY*220);ctx.stroke();ctx.strokeStyle="#ffff00";ctx.lineWidth=25;ctx.stroke();ctx.restore()}bullets.forEach(b=>{ctx.save();ctx.globalAlpha=getSplitBulletAlpha(Number(b.splitGeneration)||0);ctx.fillStyle=b.rpg?"#ff1744":b.droneBullet?"#00ff88":"#ffff00";ctx.beginPath();ctx.arc(b.x,b.y,b.size,0,Math.PI*2);ctx.fill();ctx.restore()});enemyBullets.forEach(b=>{ctx.save();ctx.globalAlpha=b.cloneBullet?.45:1;ctx.fillStyle=b.confusedBullet?"#c65cff":b.specialSplitBullet?(b.splitStage===0?"#064d18":b.splitStage===1?"#25e66b":"#8dffad"):"#ff1744";ctx.beginPath();ctx.arc(b.x,b.y,b.specialSplitBullet?b.size:6,0,Math.PI*2);ctx.fill();ctx.restore()});asteroids.forEach(a=>{
  if(a.teleportHidden&&a.teleportMarker){ctx.save();ctx.globalAlpha=.75;ctx.shadowColor="#bb66ff";ctx.shadowBlur=25;ctx.fillStyle="rgba(180,70,255,.20)";ctx.strokeStyle="#c060ff";ctx.lineWidth=4;ctx.beginPath();ctx.arc(a.teleportMarker.x,a.teleportMarker.y,a.size+18,0,Math.PI*2);ctx.fill();ctx.stroke();ctx.restore();return}
  if(difficulty==="impossible"){ctx.shadowColor="#00ffff";ctx.shadowBlur=25}else ctx.shadowBlur=0;
  if(a.clone)ctx.globalAlpha=.8;
  drawBurnEffect(a,a.size,false);
  ctx.fillStyle=a.splitClone?"#7cff9c":a.specialType==="split"?"#0b7d3b":a.specialType==="ice"?"#8eeeff":a.specialType==="gate"?"#b060ff":a.specialType==="chaser"?"#ff5533":a.specialType==="inversion"?"#42d8dc":"#777";
  ctx.beginPath();ctx.arc(a.x,a.y,a.size,0,Math.PI*2);ctx.fill();ctx.strokeStyle="#aaa";ctx.stroke();if(Number(a.poisonStrength)>0){ctx.save();ctx.strokeStyle="#7cff00";ctx.shadowColor="#7cff00";ctx.shadowBlur=16;ctx.lineWidth=3;ctx.beginPath();ctx.arc(a.x,a.y,a.size+4,0,Math.PI*2);ctx.stroke();ctx.restore()}if(a.freezeUntil>performance.now()){const freezeGlow=Number(a.freezeStrength)||0;ctx.save();ctx.globalAlpha=.55+.45*freezeGlow;ctx.strokeStyle="#9ff7ff";ctx.shadowColor="#8eeeff";ctx.shadowBlur=12+38*freezeGlow;ctx.lineWidth=3+4*freezeGlow;ctx.beginPath();ctx.arc(a.x,a.y,a.size+6+freezeGlow*4,0,Math.PI*2);ctx.stroke();ctx.restore()}drawHealthBar(a.x,a.y-a.size-10,Math.max(35,a.size*2.2),6,a.health,a.maxHealth);
});
  enemies.forEach(enemy=>{
    if(enemy.teleportHidden&&enemy.teleportMarker){ctx.save();ctx.globalAlpha=.8;ctx.shadowColor="#bf62ff";ctx.shadowBlur=25;ctx.strokeStyle="#d070ff";ctx.lineWidth=5;ctx.beginPath();ctx.arc(enemy.teleportMarker.x,enemy.teleportMarker.y,30,0,Math.PI*2);ctx.stroke();ctx.restore();return}
    const size=enemy.boss?(enemy.specialType==="split"?(enemy.splitStage===0?70:enemy.splitStage===1?55:38):55):22;
    if(difficulty==="impossible"){ctx.shadowColor="#ff00ff";ctx.shadowBlur=30}else ctx.shadowBlur=0;
    let fill=enemy.boss?(enemy.splitBoss?"#00ff66":"#ff1744"):"#9c27b0";
    if(enemy.specialType==="mirror")fill=enemy.mirrorClone?"rgba(210,190,255,.8)":"#c9a3ff";
    if(!enemy.boss&&enemy.specialType==="split"&&!enemy.splitClone)fill="#0b7d3b";if(enemy.specialType==="ice")fill="#75e6ff";if(enemy.specialType==="gate")fill="#b45cff";if(enemy.specialType==="chaser")fill="#ff5733";if(enemy.specialType==="teleport")fill="#d07aff";if(enemy.specialType==="inversion")fill="#4fe0dc";if(enemy.specialType==="doom")fill="#8b1e2d";if(enemy.splitClone)fill="#7cff9c";if(enemy.specialType==="split"&&enemy.splitStage===1)fill="#22d65d";if(enemy.specialType==="split"&&enemy.splitStage===2)fill="#7cff9c";
    if(enemy.mirrorClone)ctx.globalAlpha=.8;
    drawBurnEffect(enemy,size,true);
    ctx.fillStyle=fill;ctx.fillRect(enemy.x-size,enemy.y-size,size*2,size*2);if(isConfusedEnemy(enemy)){const confusionPower=Math.min(1,Math.max(0,Number(enemy.confusedDamageMultiplier)||getConfusionDamageMultiplier()));ctx.save();ctx.globalAlpha=.65+.35*confusionPower;ctx.strokeStyle="#d47aff";ctx.shadowColor="#c65cff";ctx.shadowBlur=18+42*confusionPower;ctx.lineWidth=3+4*confusionPower;ctx.strokeRect(enemy.x-size-5-confusionPower*6,enemy.y-size-5-confusionPower*6,size*2+10+confusionPower*12,size*2+10+confusionPower*12);ctx.font="22px Arial";ctx.textAlign="center";ctx.fillStyle="#e7b7ff";ctx.shadowBlur=12+30*confusionPower;ctx.fillText("🌀",enemy.x,enemy.y-size-18);ctx.restore()}if(Number(enemy.poisonStrength)>0){ctx.save();ctx.strokeStyle="#7cff00";ctx.shadowColor="#7cff00";ctx.shadowBlur=16;ctx.lineWidth=3;ctx.strokeRect(enemy.x-size-3,enemy.y-size-3,size*2+6,size*2+6);ctx.restore()}if(enemy.freezeUntil>performance.now()){const freezeGlow=Number(enemy.freezeStrength)||0;ctx.save();ctx.globalAlpha=.55+.45*freezeGlow;ctx.strokeStyle="#9ff7ff";ctx.shadowColor="#8eeeff";ctx.shadowBlur=12+38*freezeGlow;ctx.lineWidth=3+4*freezeGlow;ctx.strokeRect(enemy.x-size-4-freezeGlow*6,enemy.y-size-4-freezeGlow*6,size*2+8+freezeGlow*12,size*2+8+freezeGlow*12);ctx.restore()}
    ctx.globalAlpha=1;
    drawHealthBar(enemy.x,enemy.y-size-10,enemy.boss?120:50,7,enemy.health,enemy.maxHealth);
    if(enemy.boss){ctx.fillStyle="#fff";ctx.font="20px Arial";ctx.textAlign="center";ctx.fillText("BOSS",enemy.x,enemy.y+7)}
  });
  drawDeadlyLootPickups();
  // Special hazard telegraphs.
  if(activeSpecialBossArea==="teleport"||activeSpecialBossArea==="gates"){teleportAreaGates.forEach(g=>{ctx.save();ctx.globalAlpha=Math.max(0,Math.min(1,g.life/80))*.8;ctx.shadowColor="#c060ff";ctx.shadowBlur=24;ctx.strokeStyle="#d070ff";ctx.lineWidth=5;ctx.beginPath();ctx.arc(g.x,g.y,30,0,Math.PI*2);ctx.stroke();ctx.strokeStyle="#f0b0ff";ctx.lineWidth=2;ctx.beginPath();ctx.arc(g.x,g.y,20,0,Math.PI*2);ctx.stroke();ctx.restore()})}if(activeSpecialBossArea==="chaser"){window.chaserHazards=window.chaserHazards||[];window.chaserHazards.forEach(h=>{ctx.save();ctx.globalAlpha=Math.max(0,h.life/300)*.6;ctx.fillStyle="#ff2200";ctx.strokeStyle="#ff6a55";ctx.lineWidth=4;ctx.beginPath();ctx.ellipse(h.x,h.y,h.rx,h.ry,0,0,Math.PI*2);ctx.fill();ctx.stroke();ctx.restore()})}
  if(freezeTimer>0){ctx.save();ctx.globalAlpha=.18;ctx.fillStyle="#8eeeff";ctx.fillRect(0,0,canvas.width,canvas.height);ctx.restore()}drones.forEach(d=>{const size=d.type==="normal"?12:d.type==="upgraded"?16:d.type==="rpgMini"?18:22;ctx.fillStyle=d.type==="large"?"#ffd600":d.type==="rpgMini"?"#ff1744":d.type==="upgraded"?"#ff9800":"#00ff88";ctx.beginPath();ctx.arc(d.x,d.y,size,0,Math.PI*2);ctx.fill();ctx.fillStyle="#fff";ctx.font="10px Arial";ctx.textAlign="center";ctx.fillText("🛸",d.x,d.y+4)});if(swordCount>0)for(let i=0;i<swordCount;i++){const angle=swordAngle+i*Math.PI*2/swordCount,sx=player.x+Math.cos(angle)*swordRange,sy=player.y+Math.sin(angle)*swordRange;ctx.save();ctx.translate(sx,sy);ctx.rotate(angle);ctx.fillStyle="#00ffff";ctx.shadowColor="#00ffff";ctx.shadowBlur=15;ctx.fillRect(-3,-25,6,50);ctx.restore()}drawBlackHoles();drawChainExplosionEffects();explosions.forEach(e=>{ctx.strokeStyle="#ff9800";ctx.lineWidth=4;ctx.beginPath();ctx.arc(e.x,e.y,e.radius,0,Math.PI*2);ctx.stroke()});meteoriteFragments.forEach(f=>{ctx.save();ctx.globalAlpha=Math.max(0,Math.min(1,f.life/f.maxLife));ctx.translate(f.x,f.y);ctx.rotate(f.rotation);if(f.particleType){ctx.fillStyle=f.color||"#fff";ctx.shadowColor=f.color||"#fff";ctx.shadowBlur=f.particleType==="boss"?10:4;ctx.fillRect(-f.size/2,-f.size/2,f.size,f.size)}else{ctx.fillStyle=`rgb(${f.shade},${f.shade},${f.shade})`;ctx.strokeStyle="#b8b8b8";ctx.lineWidth=1;ctx.beginPath();f.shape.forEach((r,i)=>{const angle=i/f.shape.length*Math.PI*2,px=Math.cos(angle)*f.size*r,py=Math.sin(angle)*f.size*r;if(i===0)ctx.moveTo(px,py);else ctx.lineTo(px,py)});ctx.closePath();ctx.fill();ctx.stroke()}ctx.restore()});drawFloatingDamageNumbers();drawFloatingRewardsAndHealing()}
function setHud(id,show){const e=document.getElementById(id);if(e)e.style.display=show?"":"none"}
function updateMobileControlMode(){
  mobileControls.classList.toggle("joystickMode",mobileControlMode==="joystick");
  mobileControls.classList.toggle("arrowsMode",mobileControlMode==="arrows");
  if(mobileControlMode==="joystick")resetJoystick();
}
function updateMobileWeaponButtons(){
  document.querySelectorAll(".mobileWeaponBtn").forEach(btn=>{
    const w=btn.dataset.weapon;
    const unlocked=(w==="pistol"||w==="rpg"&&rpgLevel>0||w==="shotgun"&&shotgunLevel>0||w==="flamethrower"&&flamethrowerMode||w==="blackHole"&&blackHoleMaxAmmo>0);
    btn.disabled=!unlocked;
    btn.classList.toggle("locked",!unlocked);
    btn.classList.toggle("active",currentWeapon===w);
  });
}
function updateMobileControls(){
  const show=isPhoneDevice()&&mobileControlsEnabled&&gameStarted&&!paused&&!levelUpOpen&&gameRunning;
  mobileControls.style.display=show?"block":"none";
  if(!show)resetJoystick();
  updateMobileControlMode();
  updateMobileWeaponButtons();
}

levelText=document.getElementById("levelText");if(levelText)levelText.textContent="רמה "+level+" | "+Math.floor(xp)+" / "+xpNeeded+" XP";const xpBar=document.getElementById("xpBar");if(xpBar)xpBar.style.width=Math.min(100,(xp/xpNeeded)*100)+"%";const xpContainer=document.getElementById("xpContainer");if(xpContainer)xpContainer.style.display="block";updateSpeedComboUI();updateMobileWeaponButtons()}
function spendUltraModePoints(){if(!ultraModeActive)return;runUltraModeSeconds+=1/60;if(runUltraModeSeconds>=120&&!runUltraTimeAchievementChecked){runUltraTimeAchievementChecked=true;checkAchievements();}const spent=Math.min(1000/60,ultraPoints);ultraPoints=Math.max(0,ultraPoints-spent);ultraSpentForShield+=spent;while(ultraSpentForShield>=5000){ultraSpentForShield-=5000;if(ultraShields<player.maxLives){ultraShields++;showMessage("🛡️ מגן נוסף ממצב אולטרה!")}else showMessage("🛡️ הגעת למספר המגנים המרבי");updateHUD()}if(ultraPoints<=.0001){ultraPoints=0;ultraModeActive=false;ultraCooldownUntil=performance.now()+15000;ultraLastCountdown=-1;showMessage("🌈 נגמרו נקודות האולטרה — המתן 15 שניות");updateHUD()}}
function showMessage(text){const message=document.getElementById("message");message.textContent=GalaxyI18n.translate(text);message.style.opacity="1";clearTimeout(messageTimer);messageTimer=setTimeout(()=>{message.style.opacity="0"},1000)}
function getSelectedUpgradeCardTitle(upgrade,count){if(upgrade.id==="extraFire")return upgrade.title+" "+toRomanNumeral(count+1);return count<=1?upgrade.title:upgrade.title+" "+toRomanNumeral(count)}
function showSelectedCards(){const container=document.getElementById("selectedCards");container.innerHTML="";const grouped=[];const byId=new Map();selectedUpgrades.forEach(upgrade=>{let group=byId.get(upgrade.id);if(!group){group={upgrade,count:0};byId.set(upgrade.id,group);grouped.push(group)}group.count++});grouped.forEach(({upgrade,count})=>{const card=document.createElement("div");card.className="selectedCard "+upgrade.rarityClass;card.innerHTML=`<div class="icon">${upgrade.icon}</div><div class="title">${getSelectedUpgradeCardTitle(upgrade,count)}</div>`;container.appendChild(card)})}
