async function gameOver(){controlsInverted=false;inversionTimer=0;levelUpSlowMotionActive=false;updateProfileButtonVisibility();gameRunning=false;gameSettingsButton.style.display="none";mobileControls.style.display="none";document.getElementById("recordLiveBanner").style.display="none";paused=false;levelUpOpen=false;pauseMenu.style.display="none";levelUpScreen.style.display="none";document.getElementById("finalDistance").textContent=Math.floor(distance);document.getElementById("finalLevel").textContent=level;if(recordBroken){await requestRecordName();}const rank=await saveLeaderboardScore();document.getElementById("newRecordMessage").textContent=rank>=0?"🏆 "+GalaxyI18n.translate("נכנסת ללוח התוצאות במקום")+" #"+(rank+1)+"!":"";gameOverScreen.style.display="flex"}
const SLOW_MOTION_RENDER_COLLECTIONS=[()=>bullets,()=>enemyBullets,()=>asteroids,()=>enemies,()=>explosions,()=>chainExplosionEffects,()=>drones,()=>meteoriteFragments,()=>teleportAreaGates,()=>deadlyLootPickups];
let slowMotionPreviousRenderState=null;
function captureSlowMotionRenderState(){const state=[];const capture=object=>{if(!object||!Number.isFinite(object.x)||!Number.isFinite(object.y))return;state.push({object,x:object.x,y:object.y,rotation:Number.isFinite(object.rotation)?object.rotation:null,angle:Number.isFinite(object.angle)?object.angle:null})};capture(player);for(const getCollection of SLOW_MOTION_RENDER_COLLECTIONS){const collection=getCollection();if(Array.isArray(collection))for(const object of collection)capture(object)}return state}
function drawWithSlowMotionInterpolation(alpha){if(!slowMotionPreviousRenderState){draw();return}const restore=[];const amount=Math.max(0,Math.min(1,alpha));for(const previous of slowMotionPreviousRenderState){const object=previous.object;if(!object||!Number.isFinite(object.x)||!Number.isFinite(object.y))continue;restore.push({object,x:object.x,y:object.y,rotation:object.rotation,angle:object.angle});object.x=previous.x+(object.x-previous.x)*amount;object.y=previous.y+(object.y-previous.y)*amount;if(previous.rotation!==null&&Number.isFinite(object.rotation))object.rotation=previous.rotation+(object.rotation-previous.rotation)*amount;if(previous.angle!==null&&Number.isFinite(object.angle))object.angle=previous.angle+(object.angle-previous.angle)*amount}try{draw()}finally{for(const item of restore){item.object.x=item.x;item.object.y=item.y;if(item.rotation!==undefined)item.object.rotation=item.rotation;if(item.angle!==undefined)item.object.angle=item.angle}}}
function gameLoop(timestamp){animationId=null;if(!gameStarted){lastFrameTimestamp=null;updateAccumulator=0;slowMotionPreviousRenderState=null;return}const frameNow=Number.isFinite(timestamp)?timestamp:performance.now();if(lastFrameTimestamp===null)lastFrameTimestamp=frameNow;const frameDelta=Math.min(MAX_FRAME_DELTA,Math.max(0,frameNow-lastFrameTimestamp));lastFrameTimestamp=frameNow;const clockNow=performance.now();if(levelUpAnimationInGameActive){const activeOverlay=document.getElementById("levelUpAnimationOverlay");if(activeOverlay)positionLevelUpAnimationAtPlayer(activeOverlay)}let timeScale=1;if(levelUpSlowMotionActive){const elapsed=Math.max(0,clockNow-levelUpSlowMotionStartedAt),rampDuration=Math.max(1,levelUpSlowMotionDuration*.25),rampProgress=Math.min(1,elapsed/rampDuration),targetScale=Math.max(.4,Math.min(2.5,Number(levelUpAnimationSettings.gameSpeed)||1));timeScale=1+(targetScale-1)*rampProgress;if(rampProgress>=1)timeScale=targetScale}updateAccumulator+=frameDelta*timeScale;let updateSteps=0;while(updateAccumulator>=FIXED_UPDATE_STEP&&updateSteps<MAX_CATCH_UP_STEPS){if(levelUpSlowMotionActive&&timeScale<.999)slowMotionPreviousRenderState=captureSlowMotionRenderState();update();updateAccumulator-=FIXED_UPDATE_STEP;updateSteps++}updateUltraButton();if(!levelUpOpen||levelUpSlowMotionActive){if(levelUpSlowMotionActive&&timeScale<.999)drawWithSlowMotionInterpolation(updateAccumulator/FIXED_UPDATE_STEP);else draw()}const now=performance.now(),heartbeatShake=updateLowHealthHeartbeat(now);updateAmbientEffectOverlays();if(now<screenShakeUntil||heartbeatShake>0){const hitAmp=now<screenShakeUntil?screenShakePower*Math.min(1,(screenShakeUntil-now)/80+.15):0,amp=Math.max(hitAmp,heartbeatShake);canvas.style.transform=`translate(${(Math.random()*2-1)*amp}px,${(Math.random()*2-1)*amp}px)`}else{screenShakePower=0;canvas.style.transform=""}animationId=requestAnimationFrame(gameLoop)}
resizeCanvas();updateHUD();

updateProfileButtonVisibility();

   The document language may be LTR/RTL for accessibility and text shaping,
   but it must never change alignment or the direction of flex/grid layouts. */

  direction: rtl !important;
}

  unicode-bidi: plaintext;
}

===== BEGIN FILE: src/burn.js =====
"use strict";
(function(root){
const MAX_LEVEL=10,TICKS_PER_APPLICATION=8,DURATION_MS=4000;
function levelIndex(level){return Math.max(0,Math.min(MAX_LEVEL,Math.floor(Number(level)||0)))}
function getChance(level){const n=levelIndex(level);return n===0?0:Math.min(.65,Math.round((.20+(n-1)*.05)*10000)/10000)}
function getChanceDecay(level){const n=levelIndex(level);return n===0?0:Math.max(.05,Math.round((.50-(n-1)*.05)*10000)/10000)}
function getInitialDamageFraction(level){const n=levelIndex(level);return n===0?0:Math.min(.95,Math.round((.50+(n-1)*.05)*10000)/10000)}
function getDamageIncrease(level){const n=levelIndex(level);return n===0?0:Math.min(.475,Math.round((.25+(n-1)*.025)*10000)/10000)}
const api=Object.freeze({maxLevel:MAX_LEVEL,durationMs:DURATION_MS,tickIntervalMs:DURATION_MS/TICKS_PER_APPLICATION,ticksPerApplication:TICKS_PER_APPLICATION,getChance,getChanceDecay,getInitialDamageFraction,getDamageIncrease,getNextChance(previousChance,level){return Math.round(Math.max(0,Math.min(1,Number(previousChance)||0)*(1-getChanceDecay(level)))*10000)/10000},getNextDamageMultiplier(previousMultiplier,level){return Math.max(0,Number(previousMultiplier)||1)*(1+getDamageIncrease(level))},getNextDurationMs(previousDurationMs){return Math.max(0,Number(previousDurationMs)||DURATION_MS)*.75}});
if(typeof module!=="undefined"&&module.exports)module.exports=api;if(root)root.GalaxyBurn=api;
})(typeof globalThis!=="undefined"?globalThis:this);

===== END FILE: src/burn.js =====

(function(root,factory){const api=factory();if(typeof module==="object"&&module.exports)module.exports=api;if(root)root.GalaxyChainExplosion=api})(typeof window!=="undefined"?window:globalThis,function(){"use strict";const ROMAN=["I","II","III","IV","V","VI","VII","VIII","IX"];function getStats(level){const n=Math.max(1,Math.min(9,Math.floor(Number(level)||1)));return Object.freeze({level:n,roman:ROMAN[n-1],damagePercent:20+(n-1)*10,sizePercent:100+(n-1)*50,sizeMultiplier:1+(n-1)*0.5,rarity:n>=8?"מדהים":n>=5?"אגדי":"אדיר",rarityClass:n>=8?"amazing":n>=5?"legendary":"epic"})}return Object.freeze({maxLevel:9,getStats});});

===== BEGIN FILE: src/deadly-loot.js =====
"use strict";
(() => {
 const rewards=["life","shield","xp","ammo","rage"];
 function levelStats(level){const lv=Math.max(1,Math.min(9,Math.floor(Number(level)||1))),step=lv-1;return{level:lv,rageSeconds:10+step*2.5,xp:1000+step*250,shotgunAmmo:30+step*5,rpgAmmo:5+step*2}}
 function maxDrops(entity){if(entity&&entity.boss)return 6;if(entity&&entity.kind==="meteorite"){const s=Number(entity.size)||0;return s<18?0:s<24?1:2}return 2}
 function chance(level,luckLevel,doubled,boss){const steps=Math.max(0,Math.min(8,Math.floor(Number(level)||1)-1)),luck=Math.max(0,Math.floor(Number(luckLevel)||0)),base=doubled?.01:.04,card=steps*(doubled?.00125:.005),luckBonus=luck*(doubled?.000625:.0025);return(base+card+luckBonus)*(boss?2:1)}
 function rollDrops(entity,level,luckLevel,eligible,random=Math.random){const cap=maxDrops(entity);if(!cap)return[];const boss=!!(entity&&entity.boss),stats=levelStats(level),out=[];for(const kind of rewards){if(!eligible||!eligible[kind])continue;for(const doubled of[false,true])if(random()<chance(level,luckLevel,doubled,boss)){const m=doubled?2:1;out.push({kind,doubled,amount:kind==="life"||kind==="shield"?m:kind==="xp"?stats.xp*m:kind==="ammo"?{shotgun:stats.shotgunAmmo*m,rpg:stats.rpgAmmo*m}:stats.rageSeconds*m})}}for(let i=out.length-1;i>0;i--){const j=Math.floor(random()*(i+1));[out[i],out[j]]=[out[j],out[i]]}return out.slice(0,cap)}
 window.GalaxyDeadlyLoot=Object.freeze({rewards,levelStats,maxDrops,chance,rollDrops});
})();
===== END FILE: src/deadly-loot.js =====

"use strict";
(function(root){
  const RARITY_ORDER = ["common","rare","epic","legendary","amazing","ultra"];
  const RARITY_CHANCES = [
    [55,25,12,5,2.5,0.5],
    [53,24.87,12.53,5.8,3,0.8],
    [51,24.73,13.07,6.6,3.5,1.1],
    [49,24.6,13.6,7.4,4,1.4],
    [47,24.47,14.13,8.2,4.5,1.7],
    [45,24.33,14.67,9,5,2],
    [43,24.2,15.2,9.8,5.5,2.3],
    [41,24.07,15.73,10.6,6,2.6],
    [39,23.93,16.27,11.4,6.5,2.9],
    [37,23.8,16.8,12.2,7,3.2],
    [35,23.67,17.33,13,7.5,3.5],
    [33,23.53,17.87,13.8,8,3.8],
    [31,23.4,18.4,14.6,8.5,4.1],
    [29,23.27,18.93,15.4,9,4.4],
    [27,23.13,19.47,16.2,9.5,4.7],
    [25,23,20,17,10,5]
  ];
  function getRarityChances(level){
    const index=Math.max(0,Math.min(15,Math.floor(Number(level)||0)));
    const row=RARITY_CHANCES[index];
    return Object.fromEntries(RARITY_ORDER.map((rarity,i)=>[rarity,row[i]]));
  }
  function chooseUpgradeChoices(availableUpgrades,level,choiceCount=3,random=Math.random){
    const pool=[];
    const seen=new Set();
    for(const upgrade of Array.isArray(availableUpgrades)?availableUpgrades:[]){
      if(!upgrade||upgrade.id==null||seen.has(upgrade.id))continue;
      seen.add(upgrade.id);
      pool.push(upgrade);
    }
    const choices=[];
    const count=Math.max(0,Math.floor(Number(choiceCount)||0));
    while(choices.length<count&&pool.length){
      const groups=new Map();
      for(const upgrade of pool){
        const rarity=RARITY_ORDER.includes(upgrade.rarityClass)?upgrade.rarityClass:"rare";
        if(!groups.has(rarity))groups.set(rarity,[]);
        groups.get(rarity).push(upgrade);
      }
      const chances=getRarityChances(level);
      let total=0;
      for(const rarity of groups.keys())total+=chances[rarity];
      if(total<=0)break;
      let roll=Math.min(0.9999999999999999,Math.max(0,Number(random())||0))*total;
      let selectedRarity=null;
      for(const rarity of RARITY_ORDER){
        if(!groups.has(rarity))continue;
        roll-=chances[rarity];
        if(roll<0){selectedRarity=rarity;break}
      }
      if(!selectedRarity)selectedRarity=Array.from(groups.keys()).at(-1);
      const group=groups.get(selectedRarity);
      const cardIndex=Math.min(group.length-1,Math.floor(Math.max(0,Number(random())||0)*group.length));
      const selected=group[cardIndex];
      choices.push(selected);
      const index=pool.findIndex(item=>item.id===selected.id);
      if(index>=0)pool.splice(index,1);
    }
    return choices;
  }
  const api={RARITY_ORDER,RARITY_CHANCES,getRarityChances,chooseUpgradeChoices};
  if(typeof module!=="undefined"&&module.exports)module.exports=api;
  if(root)root.GalaxyLuck=api;
})(typeof window!=="undefined"?window:globalThis);

===== END FILE: src/luck.js =====

===== BEGIN FILE: src/ultra-mini-ships.js =====
/* Ultra Mini Spaceship: independent orbit, burst laser, and chained Ultra beams. */
(function () {
  "use strict";

  const MAX_LEVEL = 5;
  const POINT_COST = 3000;
  const MAX_CONNECTIONS = 20;
  const COOLDOWN_MS = 20000;
  const SHOT_INTERVAL_MS = 3000;
  const SHOT_DURATION_MS = 500;
  const STOP_DURATION_MS = 700;
  const NORMAL_DPS = 300;
  const ULTRA_DPS = 300;
  const SHIP_ORBIT_X = 112;
  const SHIP_ORBIT_Y = 72;
  const SHIP_SPEED = 0.012;
  const CHAIN_INTERVAL_MS = 400;

  let ultraMiniShipLevel = 0;
  let ultraMiniShips = [];
  let ultraMiniModeActive = false;
  let ultraMiniConnections = 0;
  let ultraMiniCooldownUntil = 0;
  let lastFrameAt = 0;
  let cardAdded = false;

  const card = {
    id: "ultraMiniShip",
    rarity: "אולטרה",
    rarityClass: "ultra",
    icon: "🛸",
    title: "אולטרה מיני חללית",
    description: "חלליות שמסתובבות סביב השחקן ויורות קרני לייזר לעבר אויבים. כל רמה מוסיפה חללית נוספת."
  };

  function addCard() {
    if (cardAdded || !Array.isArray(upgrades)) return;
    upgrades.push(card);
    cardAdded = true;
  }

  function ensureShipCount() {
    while (ultraMiniShips.length < ultraMiniShipLevel) {
      const index = ultraMiniShips.length;
      ultraMiniShips.push({
        angle: (Math.PI * 2 * index) / Math.max(1, ultraMiniShipLevel),
        fireStartedAt: 0,
        nextShotAt: performance.now() + 1000 + index * 350,
        stopUntil: 0,
        chainAt: 0,
        x: player.x,
        y: player.y,
        beams: []
      });
    }
    ultraMiniShips.forEach((ship, index) => {
      ship.slot = index;
      ship.angle = Number.isFinite(ship.angle) ? ship.angle : (Math.PI * 2 * index) / Math.max(1, ultraMiniShips.length);
    });
  }

  function nearestTarget(x, y, excluded) {
    let best = null;
    let bestDistance = Infinity;
    for (const target of [...enemies, ...asteroids]) {
      if (!target || target.health <= 0 || target.teleportHidden || (excluded && excluded.has(target))) continue;
      const distance = Math.hypot(target.x - x, target.y - y);
      if (distance < bestDistance) {
        best = target;
        bestDistance = distance;
      }
    }
    return best;
  }

  function finishUltraMiniMode(message) {
    ultraMiniModeActive = false;
    ultraModeActive = false;
    ultraMiniCooldownUntil = performance.now() + COOLDOWN_MS;
    ultraCooldownUntil = Math.max(Number(ultraCooldownUntil) || 0, performance.now() + 15000);
    ultraLastCountdown = -1;
    ultraMiniShips.forEach(ship => {
      ship.beams = [];
      ship.chainAt = 0;
    });
    showMessage(message || "🛸 הקרן ה־20 התחברה — מצב האולטרה הסתיים!");
    updateHUD();
  }

  function connectNextBeam(ship, now, impactPoint = null) {
    if (!ultraMiniModeActive || ultraMiniConnections >= MAX_CONNECTIONS) return;
    const excluded = new Set(ship.beams.filter(beam => beam.target && beam.target.health > 0).map(beam => beam.target));
    const origin = impactPoint || ship.lastImpact || (ship.beams.length ? ship.beams[ship.beams.length - 1].target : ship);
    const target = nearestTarget(origin.x, origin.y, excluded);
    if (!target) return;
    ship.beams.push({ target, connectedAt: now });
    ship.lastImpact = null;
    ultraMiniConnections += 1;
    ship.chainAt = now + CHAIN_INTERVAL_MS;
    if (ultraMiniConnections >= MAX_CONNECTIONS) finishUltraMiniMode("🛸 הקרן ה־20 התחברה — מצב האולטרה הסתיים!");
  }

  function updateShips() {
    if (!gameStarted || !gameRunning) return;
    const now = performance.now();
    const dt = lastFrameAt ? Math.min(0.05, (now - lastFrameAt) / 1000) : 1 / 60;
    lastFrameAt = now;
    ensureShipCount();

    if (ultraMiniModeActive && !ultraModeActive) {
      ultraMiniModeActive = false;
      ultraMiniShips.forEach(ship => { ship.beams = []; });
    }

    ultraMiniShips.forEach((ship, index) => {
      const firing = now < ship.stopUntil;
      if (!firing) ship.angle += SHIP_SPEED * (dt * 60);
      ship.x = player.x + Math.cos(ship.angle) * SHIP_ORBIT_X;
      ship.y = player.y + Math.sin(ship.angle) * SHIP_ORBIT_Y;

      if (ultraMiniModeActive && ultraModeActive) {
        const brokenBeam = ship.beams.find(beam => !beam.target || beam.target.health <= 0);
        if (brokenBeam && brokenBeam.target) ship.lastImpact = { x: brokenBeam.target.x, y: brokenBeam.target.y };
        ship.beams = ship.beams.filter(beam => beam.target && beam.target.health > 0);
        ship.beams.forEach(beam => {
          const damage = ULTRA_DPS * dt;
          beam.target.health -= damage;
          if (damage > 0) showDamageNumber(beam.target, damage);
          if (beam.target.health <= 0) {
            ship.lastImpact = { x: beam.target.x, y: beam.target.y };
            if (enemies.includes(beam.target)) destroyEnemy(beam.target);
            else if (asteroids.includes(beam.target)) destroyAsteroid(beam.target);
          }
        });
        const brokenDuringDamage = ship.beams.some(beam => !beam.target || beam.target.health <= 0);
        if (brokenBeam || brokenDuringDamage) {
          const impactPoint = ship.lastImpact;
          ship.beams = ship.beams.filter(beam => beam.target && beam.target.health > 0);
          connectNextBeam(ship, now, impactPoint);
        } else if (ship.lastImpact && now >= ship.chainAt) {
          connectNextBeam(ship, now, ship.lastImpact);
        } else if (now >= ship.chainAt && ultraMiniConnections < MAX_CONNECTIONS) {
          connectNextBeam(ship, now);
        }
        return;
      }

      if (now >= ship.nextShotAt && now >= ship.stopUntil) {
        const target = nearestTarget(ship.x, ship.y);
        if (target) {
          ship.fireStartedAt = now;
          ship.stopUntil = now + STOP_DURATION_MS;
          ship.nextShotAt = now + SHOT_INTERVAL_MS;
          ship.burstTarget = target;
        } else {
          ship.nextShotAt = now + 250;
        }
      }

      if (ship.burstTarget && now < ship.fireStartedAt + SHOT_DURATION_MS && ship.burstTarget.health > 0) {
        const damage = NORMAL_DPS * dt;
        ship.burstTarget.health -= damage;
        if (damage > 0) showDamageNumber(ship.burstTarget, damage);
        if (ship.burstTarget.health <= 0) {
          if (enemies.includes(ship.burstTarget)) destroyEnemy(ship.burstTarget);
          else if (asteroids.includes(ship.burstTarget)) destroyAsteroid(ship.burstTarget);
          ship.burstTarget = null;
        }
      } else if (now >= ship.fireStartedAt + SHOT_DURATION_MS) {
        ship.burstTarget = null;
      }
    });
  }

  function drawShips() {
    if (!gameStarted || !ultraMiniShips.length) return;
    const now = performance.now();
    ctx.save();
    ultraMiniShips.forEach(ship => {
      if (ultraMiniModeActive && ultraModeActive) {
        ship.beams.forEach((beam, index) => {
          const target = beam.target;
          if (!target || target.health <= 0) return;
          const hue = (now / 8 + index * 35) % 360;
          ctx.save();
          ctx.strokeStyle = `hsl(${hue},100%,65%)`;
          ctx.shadowColor = ctx.strokeStyle;
          ctx.shadowBlur = 15;
          ctx.lineWidth = 4;
          ctx.beginPath();
          ctx.moveTo(ship.x, ship.y);
          ctx.lineTo(target.x, target.y);
          ctx.stroke();
          ctx.restore();
        });
      } else if (ship.burstTarget && now < ship.fireStartedAt + SHOT_DURATION_MS && ship.burstTarget.health > 0) {
        ctx.save();
        ctx.strokeStyle = "#62f7ff";
        ctx.shadowColor = "#62f7ff";
        ctx.shadowBlur = 18;
        ctx.lineWidth = 5;
        ctx.beginPath();
        ctx.moveTo(ship.x, ship.y);
        ctx.lineTo(ship.burstTarget.x, ship.burstTarget.y);
        ctx.stroke();
        ctx.restore();
      }
      ctx.save();
      ctx.translate(ship.x, ship.y);
      ctx.rotate(ship.angle + Math.PI / 2);
      ctx.shadowColor = ultraMiniModeActive ? "#ff4df0" : "#67f7ff";
      ctx.shadowBlur = ultraMiniModeActive ? 22 : 12;
      ctx.fillStyle = ultraMiniModeActive ? "#f4b5ff" : "#75f7ff";
      ctx.beginPath();
      ctx.moveTo(0, -15);
      ctx.lineTo(-10, 10);
      ctx.lineTo(0, 6);
      ctx.lineTo(10, 10);
      ctx.closePath();
      ctx.fill();
      ctx.restore();
    });
    ctx.restore();
  }

  function updateButton() {
    const button = document.getElementById("ultraMiniShipButton");
    if (!button) return;
    const now = performance.now();
    const remaining = Math.max(0, Math.ceil((ultraMiniCooldownUntil - now) / 1000));
    const visible = ultraUnlocked && ultraMiniShipLevel > 0 && gameStarted && gameOverScreen.style.display !== "flex";
    const unavailable = !ultraMiniModeActive && (remaining > 0 || ultraPoints < POINT_COST || !gameRunning);
    button.style.display = visible ? "flex" : "none";
    button.classList.toggle("active", ultraMiniModeActive);
    button.classList.toggle("disabled", unavailable);
    button.disabled = unavailable;
    button.innerHTML = GalaxyI18n.translate("🛸 מיני חללית אולטרה") + "<br><small>" +
      (ultraMiniModeActive ? ultraMiniConnections + " / 20 " + GalaxyI18n.translate("קרניים") :
        remaining ? "⏳ " + remaining + " " + GalaxyI18n.translate("שניות") :
          Math.floor(ultraPoints).toLocaleString("en-US") + " / 3,000 " + GalaxyI18n.translate("נקודות")) + "</small>";
  }

  const originalGetUpgradeCardTitle = getUpgradeCardTitle;
  getUpgradeCardTitle = function (upgrade) {
    if (upgrade.id === "ultraMiniShip") return upgrade.title + " " + ["I", "II", "III", "IV", "V"][ultraMiniShipLevel];
    return originalGetUpgradeCardTitle(upgrade);
  };

  const originalApplyUpgrade = applyUpgrade;
  applyUpgrade = function (upgrade) {
    originalApplyUpgrade(upgrade);
    if (upgrade.id === "ultraMode") addCard();
    if (upgrade.id === "ultraMiniShip" && ultraMiniShipLevel < MAX_LEVEL) {
      ultraMiniShipLevel += 1;
      ensureShipCount();
      showMessage("🛸 אולטרה מיני חללית " + ["I", "II", "III", "IV", "V"][ultraMiniShipLevel - 1] + " נפתחה!");
    }
  };

  const originalOpenLevelUp = openLevelUp;
  openLevelUp = function () {
    if (ultraUnlocked && ultraMiniShipLevel < MAX_LEVEL) addCard();
    else if (ultraMiniShipLevel >= MAX_LEVEL) {
      const index = upgrades.findIndex(upgrade => upgrade.id === "ultraMiniShip");
      if (index >= 0) upgrades.splice(index, 1);
    }
    originalOpenLevelUp();
  };

  const originalUpdate = update;
  update = function () {
    originalUpdate();
    updateShips();
    updateButton();
  };

  const originalDraw = draw;
  draw = function () {
    originalDraw();
    drawShips();
  };

  const originalSpendUltraModePoints = spendUltraModePoints;
  spendUltraModePoints = function () {
    if (ultraMiniModeActive) return;
    originalSpendUltraModePoints();
  };

  const originalUpdateHUD = updateHUD;
  updateHUD = function () {
    originalUpdateHUD();
    updateButton();
  };

  document.getElementById("ultraMiniShipButton").addEventListener("click", function () {
    if (ultraMiniModeActive) {
      ultraMiniModeActive = false;
      ultraModeActive = false;
      ultraCooldownUntil = performance.now() + 15000;
      ultraMiniCooldownUntil = performance.now() + COOLDOWN_MS;
      showMessage("🛸 מצב אולטרה מיני חללית כובה");
      updateHUD();
      return;
    }
    if (!ultraUnlocked || ultraMiniShipLevel <= 0 || !gameRunning) return;
    if (performance.now() < ultraMiniCooldownUntil) return;
    if (ultraPoints < POINT_COST) {
      showMessage("נדרשות 3,000 נקודות אולטרה");
      return;
    }
    ultraPoints -= POINT_COST;
    ultraModeActive = true;
    ultraMiniModeActive = true;
    ultraMiniConnections = 0;
    ultraMiniShips.forEach((ship, index) => {
      ship.beams = [];
      ship.chainAt = performance.now() + index * 100;
    });
    showMessage("🛸 אולטרה מיני חללית הופעלה!");
    updateHUD();
  });

  window.GalaxyUltraMiniShips = { isActive: () => ultraMiniModeActive };
  updateButton();
})();

===== END FILE: src/ultra-mini-ships.js =====
